**Session summary, 2026-08-28 (new session) — Developer raw API tester (DEV-1/2, MUI-11) grilled and built as [ADR-054](./adr.md), on `feature/developer-api-tester`.** Closed item 17 from the prior pointer — the one remaining real gap in the Middleware Panel row, deliberately held back from ADR-052's batch for its own grilling on sandbox-only enforcement, production-mode blocking, and its own audit trail. Investigation before grilling found the supporting infrastructure already existed almost entirely — `SupplierConfigSchema`'s secret-field classification, `SupplierRequestPayloadRedactor`, `SupplierRequestLogger` (all ADR-051), and each adapter's own sandbox/testing `api_config` flag (ADR-046) — so the grilling focused on how to wire them together safely, not on building new primitives.

Real design work, all pinned before code: the "raw JSON editor" edits a typed DTO's fields (`SupplierOrderRequest`/`SupplierStatusCheckRequest`), never a body forwarded to the supplier as-is, keeping `SupplierAdapter` the stable seam it already is; dry-run (default on) is evaluated entirely server-side with zero network calls, only turning it off actually dispatches; `createOrder` is hard-blocked (not just confirm-dialoged) when live-fired against a supplier not confirmed sandbox/testing, since unlike the other 4 (read-only) methods it can create a real, un-tracked obligation on the supplier's side; a `createOrder` test always gets a server-generated, non-editable `DEVTEST-` reference, structurally unable to collide with a real order's `REF-` sequence. New `App\Services\Supplier\RequestLog\DeveloperTestContext` (a request-scoped ambient flag, `runIn()`/`active()`) lets `SupplierRequestLogger` prefix a real call's `call_type` with `dev_test_` without adding a parameter to any `SupplierAdapter` method — the interface itself is untouched. `SupplierConfigSchema::isSandbox()` was extracted from a private `SupplierController` method (unchanged behavior) so both controllers share one sandbox-flag lookup.

Backend: `Middleware\DeveloperToolController::test()`, `TestSupplierAdapterRequest` (conditional-on-`method` validation), all 5 `SupplierAdapter` methods exposed. Admin: `/middleware/developer-tools` (supplier + method selects, conditional payload fields, dry-run checkbox, request/response JSON viewer), nav link un-greyed. 7 new controller tests + 1 new adapter-level test (`dev_test_` prefix), full backend suite **974/974 green**. `npx tsc --noEmit`/`eslint`/`next build` clean on `admin/`.

**Live-verified 2026-08-28 (dev servers already running)** — a throwaway `super_admin` QA account (deleted after) confirmed: page renders with the pre-existing "Developer / API Tester" nav link now live; selecting the real `Gamevion` supplier shows its Configured/Sandbox badges plus the static per-adapter caveat text; dry-run preview for `checkBalance` correctly shows "(no request body for this call)"; **live (non-dry-run) `checkBalance` against the real Gamevion sandbox API succeeded**, returning the real account balance; that exact call appeared in `/middleware/request-logs` as `dev_test_checkBalance`, redacted, distinguishable from ordinary `checkBalance` rows above it — confirming the `DeveloperTestContext` prefixing end-to-end, not just at the unit level; `createOrder` dry-run preview showed the built request with a `DEVTEST-…` `referenceNumber`, never editable. The production-mode block for a live `createOrder` was not exercised live (would have required temporarily flipping the founder's real, active Gamevion supplier config) — covered instead by a deterministic backend test (`test_live_create_order_is_blocked_when_supplier_is_in_production_mode`).

**Session summary, 2026-08-28 (new session, `fix/storefront-branding-and-ui`) — `social_links` schema-drift fix (pointer item 15) shipped, expanded to 5 platforms.** Root cause (found 2026-08-27, not yet fixed as of the last pointer): `BrandingController::show()` defaulted a missing `social_links` to PHP `[]`, which `json_encode`s as a JSON *array*; the storefront's zod schema (`BrandingWireSchema`) expects an object or `null`, so every request with no branding saved yet failed validation, logged repeatedly via `/client-errors`. Fixed as `?: null` instead of `?? []` (`BrandingController.php:71`). Same session, founder asked to also add TikTok/YouTube/WhatsApp alongside the existing Facebook/Instagram (`social_links` is a free-form `json` column, ADR-028 — no migration needed): `UpdateBrandingRequest` gained the 3 new validation rules; storefront's `BrandingWireSchema`/`Branding` interface and admin's `settings.ts` `Branding` type extended; `StoreBrandingSection.tsx` gained 3 new URL fields. New `storefront/src/components/icons/SocialIcons.tsx` — 5 official brand-mark SVGs (mono variant, CC0-1.0, sourced from thesvg.org) inlined as local components, replacing `@phosphor-icons/react`'s generic Facebook/Instagram glyphs in `SiteFooter.tsx`; kept as a bundled asset like every other icon in this app, not a runtime CDN fetch. **ADR-038 opportunistic migration applied while already touching `StoreBrandingSection.tsx`** (founder's own reasoning: this screen is unlikely to be revisited soon, so defer-migrating it risks it staying on TailAdmin primitives indefinitely) — its `Button` swapped to the PrimeReact-Tailwind wrapper (`components/ui/button`, already the established target). `Label`/`Input` deliberately left as-is: unlike Button/Select/DataTable/Dialog/Tag, `components/form`'s Input has no established PrimeReact-Tailwind target component anywhere in this codebase yet (per the PrimeReact Migration Tracker's own "lower priority" note) — inventing one for a single file would be a new, undecided seam, not a same-shape swap. New regression test (`test_show_returns_null_social_links_when_not_set`), 8/8 `BrandingControllerTest` green, 11/11 `SettingsControllerTest` green. `admin/`+`storefront/` `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint` both clean. **Live-verified** against the real local dev DB (no browser login performed — automated password entry is a hard-disallowed action in this environment; test data seeded/cleared via `php artisan tinker` instead, `BrandingController::forgetCache()` called to bust the 60s cache): confirmed the pre-fix null-branding state no longer logs a schema-drift warning, then confirmed all 5 icons render correctly in the footer with real URLs before the throwaway `ResellerBranding` row was deleted and the cache re-busted back to the original empty-DB state.

**Session summary, 2026-08-28 (same session, `fix/storefront-branding-and-ui`) — full `Table`→`DataTable` migration (ADR-038) across all 21 admin screens/components that used the old primitive, closing that row in the PrimeReact Migration Tracker.** Founder's own call, deliberately scoped wider than the opportunistic default (AGENTS.md's stated policy is "migrate a screen only when already touching it for another reason") since the founder judged these screens unlikely to be revisited soon otherwise. Split by risk: 6 money/fraud-adjacent files (`admin/orders`, `admin/vouchers`, `admin/withdrawals`, `admin/blacklist`, `DeliveryLogsTable.tsx`, `BlacklistHitsModal.tsx`) done by hand in the main session with individual `tsc`/live-browser verification each; the remaining 15 (games, hero-slides, reviews, all 4 SEO list screens, users, payment-methods, price-sync, product-manager, sandbox, and 3 price-sync sub-components) delegated to a background agent under an explicit written spec (exact structural transform, the two in-repo reference examples to copy from, and a hard boundary: Modal/Dropdown/`components/form` explicitly out of scope). Opportunistically, per ADR-038's own trigger, every touched file also had its `Badge`→`Tag` and old `Button`→new `Button` swapped in the same pass (color/severity and size/variant mapping pinned once, reused everywhere — see the money-critical files' own diffs for the exact mapping table). Two non-obvious PrimeReact behaviors confirmed correct, not assumed: `DataTableTBody`'s children is a render-prop (`{({item}) => ...}`) receiving one row at a time, with the array itself moving up to `DataTable`'s own `data` prop — a real structural change, not just an element rename; and `dataKey` supports dot-path field resolution (verified by reading `@primeuix/utils`' `resolveFieldData` source directly, not assumed), used for `SyncDetailsModal.tsx`'s `dataKey="package.id"` (that row shape has no top-level id) and `product-manager/page.tsx`'s `dataKey="category_raw"` (a nullable grouping key, not a synthetic id). Coordinating session independently re-ran `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint`/`npm run build` after the subagent's work (all clean, identical 37-route list) and spot-verified 5 of the 15 delegated screens live in a browser (users, games incl. its second package-detail table, product-manager's all 3 tables, price-sync incl. its Sync History + `SyncDetailsModal`, sandbox, one SEO screen) rather than trusting the subagent's own report at face value. Zero files in the entire `admin/` app still import the old `Table` primitive. `Badge`/`Button` are now partially migrated (only in files that also had a Table) — 2 and 28 files respectively still use the old primitives, both now `🟡 Partially migrated` in the tracker rather than `⬜ Not migrated`. `Modal`/`Dropdown`/`components/form` untouched, as scoped.

**Session summary, 2026-08-28 (same session, `fix/storefront-branding-and-ui`) — PrimeReact primary color fixed + full `Modal`→`Dialog` migration (ADR-038), closing that row in the tracker.** Founder noticed default-severity buttons rendering flat gray instead of blue while reviewing the Table migration's output and asked for the important/destructive-action color distinction to actually read (blue for the primary action, red for destructive, white/outlined for Cancel) — root cause was `admin/src/app/globals.css`'s `--p-primary-*` scale, left at PrimeReact's default `oklch(*, 0, 0)` (zero chroma, pure grayscale) placeholder since ADR-038 shipped; its own code comment had named "once PrimeReact sits next to brand-colored UI" as the revisit trigger, which the Table migration's 21 screens had just reached. Fixed by wiring `--p-primary-*` directly to this app's existing `--color-brand-*` scale (a themeless swap — same tier structure, 11 shades each, no new color chosen); `severity="danger"` buttons were already correctly solid red via `Button`'s own hardcoded compound variants, unaffected. Live-verified in both light and dark mode before proceeding.

With buttons now correctly colored, the founder asked to migrate `Modal`→`Dialog` next. This app had no established form-modal Dialog pattern yet (only Backups' confirm-style dialog existed) — built one from scratch on the first file (`CreateBlacklistEntryModal.tsx`): `DialogHeader` with a `DialogTitle` + `DialogClose` (new `CloseIcon` in `@/icons`, same glyph the old `Modal` used) in `DialogHeaderActions`, form content in `DialogContent`, Cancel kept `variant="outlined"` and the primary action kept solid-default (or `severity="danger"` for destructive ones) — matching the founder's color ask exactly. **A real automation-environment quirk was found and diagnosed while verifying this first file, not a code bug:** `computer`-tool clicks on the trigger button often failed to visibly open the dialog, while a raw `element.click()` call worked every time. Root-caused via DOM inspection (not guessed) — the popup *was* mounting correctly (`data-open` present, `opacity-100` class present, no `data-enter-from`/`data-leave-to` blocking it) but stayed at `opacity: 0`; `document.visibilityState` read `"hidden"` for the automated tab, and Chrome throttles `requestAnimationFrame`-driven CSS transitions (which PrimeReact's enter/leave animation system depends on) for backgrounded/hidden tabs — a real user's foregrounded tab would never hit this. Verification for the rest of the session used DOM-attribute checks (`data-open`, resolved classes, rendered form-field/button counts) as the primary evidence, with screenshots taken opportunistically when they happened to render.

Split by risk, same strategy as the Table migration: the 8 money/fraud-adjacent modals (`CreateBlacklistEntryModal`, `BlacklistHitsModal`, `IssueVoucherModal`, `MarkDeliveredModal`, `ResendDeliveryModal`, `CreateVoucherModal`, `MergeVouchersModal`, `WithdrawalRequestModal`) migrated by hand with individual `tsc` + live verification each; the remaining 13 (reviews' bulk-approve confirm, game/package edit+delete, hero slides, 4 middleware modals, 3 SEO save modals, price-sync's view-only details modal) delegated to a background agent under a written spec naming three concrete in-repo reference patterns to copy (form-modal, confirm-style with `DialogFooter`, view-only detail) rather than describing the shape in prose. Two files (`EditGameModal.tsx`/`EditPackageModal.tsx`) didn't cleanly match any of the three reference patterns — each has an internal `confirmingDelete` boolean that swaps the dialog's *body* between the edit form and an inline delete-confirmation view, not a second nested Dialog; the agent flagged this rather than forcing a fit, and the coordinating session spot-verified the resulting code directly (title stayed static in the outer `DialogTitle`, both inner states kept their existing conditional rendering, `severity="danger"` correctly applied to the destructive "Delete Game" button only). Opportunistically, old `Button`→new `Button` was swapped in every touched file across both batches (no old `Badge` usage existed in the 13-file batch). Coordinating session independently re-ran `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint`/`npm run build` after the subagent's work (all clean, identical 37-route list) and read the full diff of 3 of the more structurally-unusual files (`EditGameModal.tsx`, `SyncDetailsModal.tsx`'s dynamic title, `admin/reviews/page.tsx`'s confirm-style `DialogFooter`) rather than trusting the subagent's own report at face value. Zero files in `admin/` still import the old `Modal` primitive. `Button` is now down to 10 files (from 28 before this session), `Badge` unchanged at 2 (neither batch touched a file using it) — both remain `🟡 Partially migrated`; `Dropdown`/`components/form` untouched, as scoped.

**Session summary, 2026-08-29 (same session, `fix/storefront-branding-and-ui`) — remaining PrimeReact migration closed: `Dropdown`, and the last of `Badge`/`Button`.** Founder asked to finish the tracker's baki (remainder) outright rather than wait for further opportunistic triggers. `Dropdown` had no established target component yet (unlike Table/Modal, which already had `datatable.tsx`/`dialog.tsx` built before this session started) — built `admin/src/components/ui/popover.tsx` from scratch, following the same `cn()`-wrapped PrimeReact-Tailwind pattern as the existing wrappers. Chose `Popover` over `Menu`: the only real consumer, `UserDropdown.tsx`, renders a static name/email header block plus a single "Sign out" action, not a navigable item list, so `Popover`'s free-form composition (`PopoverHeader`/`PopoverContent`, mirroring `Dialog`'s shape) fit without forcing a menu-item abstraction the content doesn't need. Anchored via `Popover`'s `anchor` prop (an `HTMLElement`) rather than wrapping the trigger in `PopoverTrigger`, to keep the existing plain `<button>` unchanged and stay consistent with how every `Dialog` in this app is already driven by external `open`/`onOpenChange` state rather than a `*Trigger` sub-component. **Real lint catch, not styling:** the first cut read `triggerRef.current` directly in the render body to pass as `anchor` — `eslint`'s `react-hooks/refs` rule correctly flagged this (a ref's `.current` is not safe to read during render, only in effects/handlers); fixed by holding the element in `useState` via a ref-callback (`ref={setTriggerEl}`) instead of `useRef()`, avoiding the render-time ref read entirely. `Badge`'s last 2 files (`middleware/page.tsx`, `admin/seo/page.tsx`) and `Button`'s last 10 (mostly Settings/SEO screens and two middleware components) were migrated directly — no other primitive migration was going to touch them opportunistically, so the founder chose to close them now rather than leave them waiting indefinitely. Same color-mapping rules as every prior pass (outlined for neutral/Cancel, solid default for the primary action, `severity="danger"` for destructive). `admin/` `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint`/`npm run build` all clean (37 routes, unchanged). Live-verified: `middleware/page.tsx`'s supplier-health and API-call-log `Tag`s render correctly (green "Healthy"/"success"), and `UserDropdown`'s popover opens with the correct name/email/Sign-out content (confirmed via DOM inspection — the same automation tab-visibility quirk documented in the Modal migration entry above meant a screenshot alone wasn't reliable evidence here either). Every hand-rolled TailAdmin primitive from the original ADR-038 tracker is now migrated except `components/form`, which stays deliberately deferred (no chosen target, lower priority, current inputs work).

**Founder live-testing, same day — real bug found across every migrated Dialog/Popover, not just one screen.** Opening `HeroSlideModal` (Edit) showed a popup with no visible title or close button at all — the content started right at "Eyebrow (optional)" flush against the app's own sticky header. Root cause: this app's `AppHeader` (`src/layout/AppHeader.tsx`) is `sticky z-99999`; PrimeReact assigns each open overlay its own z-index via **inline style** (base 1100 for modals, 1000 for overlays/menus, incrementing per open instance) — inline style always beats a Tailwind class on the component regardless of specificity tricks, so the first fix attempt (raising `DialogPositioner`/`PopoverPositioner`'s className z-index) was silently inert; confirmed via `getComputedStyle` showing `z-index: 1102` on the positioner despite a `z-[100000]` class present. Every Dialog and the one Popover (`UserDropdown`) had their header/title rendering underneath the app header the whole time — just invisible until a tall enough modal (or the right viewport) made it obvious. Fixed at the source instead: `PrimeReactProvider`'s `zIndex` config (`components/prime-provider.tsx`) raises the base values (`modal`/`tooltip` 100100, `overlay`/`menu` 100000) so every overlay type shifts above the header together, preserving PrimeReact's own relative-stacking behavior for nested overlays (e.g. a future PrimeReact `Select` opened from inside a `Dialog`) rather than hardcoding one layer's z-index and breaking that. The two components' className-level z-index attempts were reverted (inert, would have confused a future reader) once the real fix landed. Live-verified: `getComputedStyle` on the positioner now reads `100102` vs. the header's `99999`, and `HeroSlideModal`'s title/close button render correctly above the header in a real screenshot.

**Session summary, 2026-08-29 (same session, `fix/storefront-branding-and-ui`) — `components/form` migrated, closing ADR-038's PrimeReact migration end to end; dead old primitives deleted.** Founder asked to finish the tracker's last piece outright. No PrimeReact-Tailwind wrapper existed yet for `Label`/`Input`/`Select` (unlike Table/Modal, which already had targets before this session). Built `components/ui/label.tsx` and `input.tsx` wrapping PrimeReact's `Label`/`InputText`, keeping the exact same flat prop surface as the old primitives so most of the 25 call sites needed only the import swapped. **Real TypeScript gap found and fixed in `input.tsx`:** `InputTextProps` derives `onChange`'s event-parameter type from a polymorphic `ExtractProps<T>` (`T` defaults to the broad `React.ElementType`) that resolves too loosely, surfacing as `Parameter 'e' implicitly has an 'any' type` at every consumer — fixed by explicitly re-declaring `onChange?: React.ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` in `input.tsx`'s own prop interface rather than annotating each call site. For `Select`, rather than composing the 9 granular parts (`Select`/`SelectTrigger`/`SelectValue`/.../`SelectOption`) by hand at every one of the 9 files still needing it, promoted the same flat options/value/onChange convenience shape `admin/reports/page.tsx`'s own local `FilterSelect` had already proven out into a shared `SimpleSelect` export on `select.tsx` — a deliberate deepening of that module rather than repeating the boilerplate 9 more times.

Split by risk, same strategy as every prior pass: `CreateBlacklistEntryModal.tsx` built and live-verified by hand first as the reference pattern (real create through the new `Label`+`Input`+`SimpleSelect`, row landed in the table) before touching anything else; the 6 money/fraud-adjacent forms (`IssueVoucherModal`, `MarkDeliveredModal`, `ResendDeliveryModal`, `CreateVoucherModal`, `MergeVouchersModal`, `WithdrawalRequestModal`) migrated by hand, `ResendDeliveryModal`'s package-picker `Select`→`SimpleSelect` live-verified end-to-end on a real sandbox test order (temporarily flipped to `failed` via tinker to unlock the Resend action, reverted after); the remaining 19 lower-risk files delegated to a background agent under a written spec naming the two hand-migrated files as reference examples. Coordinating session independently re-ran `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint`/`npm run build` after the agent's work (all clean, identical 37-route list), grepped for zero remaining old-primitive imports, and spot-verified the two files with multiple `Select` usages (`LinkCategoryModal.tsx`, `CreateSandboxOrderModal.tsx`, 2 each) plus a live browser check of `EditGameModal.tsx` against real data (Name/Slug/Category/Image URL fields, Player ID Validator `SimpleSelect` correctly pre-filled from the record) rather than trusting the agent's own report at face value.

**With every hand-rolled TailAdmin primitive confirmed to have zero remaining consumers app-wide** (`Table`, `Modal`, `Dropdown`, `Badge`, `Button`, `components/form` — verified by exhaustive grep, not assumed from the migration history alone), deleted the 8 now-dead old primitive files/directories (`components/ui/{table,modal,dropdown,badge,button}`, `components/form`) in the same commit rather than leave orphaned code sitting in the tree. `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint`/`npm run build` re-confirmed clean after the deletion (37 routes, unchanged). Only `RichTextEditor` remains on the tracker, permanently exempt — no PrimeReact rich-text equivalent exists. ADR-038's PrimeReact migration is closed.

**Session summary, 2026-08-29 (new session, `feature/vip-membership-config-grill` then `feature/vip-membership-implementation`, stacked) — VIP Membership (ADR-027) grilled to a build-ready mechanism and built through Phase 5.** Founder asked to make the tier fee/quota/discount% admin-editable rather than hardcoded; grilling that one question widened into three real gaps ADR-027's original design had left open — recorded as [ADR-027's 2026-08-29 addendum](./adr.md), items 14-29 — before any code: the admin-config service boundary (new `MembershipPricingService`, live-computed, never a stored snapshot), the session/dashboard access mechanism (a 30-day signed token, `/membership` storefront page), and the OTP vendor+channel (moved from WhatsApp to email/Plunk — WhatsApp Business API's real per-message cost, checked live, made it the wrong fit for a recurring per-30-day verification; Telegram was evaluated and rejected outright, not just deprioritized, since its Bot API structurally can't message a user who hasn't first messaged the bot). Full reasoning and the cost comparison sources are in the addendum itself, not duplicated here.

Built test-first (TDD, matching this project's established practice for money-adjacent logic), phase by phase, each live-verified against a real running stack before moving on — not just `tsc`/`eslint`/the test suite:

- **Phase 1 (schema):** `membership_plans` (fixed 2 rows, seeded with draft numbers — still not the founder's real fee/quota/discount%), `memberships` (email-keyed, not phone — decision 27; became `(reseller_id, email)`-keyed in ADR-061 PR-B), `membership_plan_changes` (audit trail), `PlatformSettings.membership_enabled` (the pre-launch kill switch), `MembershipPricingService`.
- **Phase 2 (`/admin/membership` config UI):** edit-only against the two fixed tiers, cross-tier discount validation, audit log write per changed field. **Founder follow-up ask, same session:** a "Preview Pricing Impact" button was added — previews the *unsaved* form value against one real sample package, showing the markup% breakdown itself (package markup → discount% → effective markup, not just the final RM numbers) plus the margin given up per sale, reusing `MembershipPricingService::effectiveMarkupPercent()` (extracted as its own method specifically so the preview can never drift from what checkout actually charges).
- **Phase 3 (public catalog wiring):** `member_price_sen` added to `CatalogController`'s public package response, gated on the kill switch. The packages cache moved to its own Redis-tagged store (`config('cache.catalog_packages_store')`, defaulting to the `cache` Redis connection `ADR-020` already provisioned — not a new dependency) so a tier edit invalidates every game's cache in one call; a real bug was caught and fixed before it shipped, not after — `CatalogController::forgetPackagesCache()`'s existing `Cache::forget()` call would have silently missed the now-tagged entries. New `config('cache.catalog_packages_store')` env var, overridden to `array` in both PHPUnit configs so the fast sqlite suite never needs a real Redis connection. Confirmed via real regression tests (not assumed) that both existing markup-edit paths — `PackageController::updateMarkup` and `SettingsController::bulkMarkup` — correctly feed into `member_price_sen`, since it's computed live from `Package.markup_percent`, never a snapshot.
- **Phase 4 (identity):** email OTP (`OtpService` — hashed, 10-minute expiry, 5-attempt lockout independent of the request throttle), a stateless 30-day session token (`MembershipSessionTokenService`, `Crypt`-signed, no new session table), and `PlunkMailer` built against Plunk's real API reference (confirmed live 2026-08-29, not assumed: `POST {base_url}/v1/send`, Bearer auth). `config('services.plunk')` is a new external dependency with no real key yet — same "config placeholder" pattern Xendit/Gamevion started with. **Live-verified against Plunk's real API, not a fake:** a real request reached `next-api.useplunk.com` and Plunk's own real error responses were received in sequence as the founder provisioned the key — first `401 MISSING_AUTH` (confirmed the request/auth-header shape), then `422` "Sender email is required" once the key was added. **Founder's own call: defer sender-domain verification, not block on it** — the rest of the flow (OTP generate/verify/session-token issuance) is proven correct via `tinker`-bypassed testing instead.
- **Phase 5 (`/membership` storefront page):** email → OTP → dashboard, one entry point for both a new subscriber and an existing member. A custom 6-box OTP input (storefront has no component library — confirmed before building, matched existing convention rather than introducing shadcn/ui for one field). `useMembershipToken()` (`useSyncExternalStore`) mirrors admin's own `useClientSession()` hook exactly, including catching the same `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` trap this codebase has hit before (ADR-038 decision 8) — caught by lint during this build too, fixed by deriving the visible step from token/dashboard state instead of storing it separately. Deliberately `localStorage`, not admin's `sessionStorage` — decision 23's whole point is surviving across visits. **Real bug found and fixed during live verification:** the test storefront dev server was started on port 3010, silently CORS-blocked (`config/cors.php` only allows `:3000`/`:3001`) — not a code bug, just the wrong port; switching fixed it. Full flow proven end-to-end on the real stack: email step renders and posts correctly (Plunk failure surfaces as a clean user-facing error, not a crash); a real session token obtained via the backend directly (OTP generated via `tinker` to bypass the deferred Plunk block) was set into the browser's own `localStorage`, and the dashboard shell — "verified, no active membership yet" + empty order history — rendered correctly, with "Sign out" correctly clearing the token. The OTP-input box UI itself is code-reviewed/type-checked but not click-tested live, since reaching it through the real flow needs the same deferred Plunk step.

No **storefront** nav link added in this session — the kill switch stays off, so the customer-facing `/membership` page is unreachable from the storefront's own navigation, matching the "invisible until launch" intent decision 20 was built for. (The admin sidebar *does* link `/admin/membership` — added with Phase 2's config UI, `b01546a` — deliberately, since Q14 keeps the admin registry usable pre-launch.) **Superseded 2026-09-01 (ADR-064):** the storefront nav now shows a "Membership" link, but *conditionally* — only when `GET /api/membership/plans` is non-empty (membership effectively enabled for the brand), so the "invisible until launch" intent is preserved and enforced by data, not by omitting the link. 1025/1025 backend suite green (up from 979 at session start); `admin/` and `storefront/` `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint`/`npm run build` all clean throughout. **Not built yet, explicitly out of this session's scope:** Phase 6 (checkout-time personalization + race-safe quota decrement, reusing `LedgerService`/`VoucherService`'s existing row-lock pattern) and Phase 7 (WhatsApp renewal reminder — vendor, Meta Cloud API vs. a BSP, still unpicked). **Founder's own next steps:** provision Plunk's sender-domain verification before Phase 4's email delivery can be live-tested end-to-end; supply real fee/quota/discount% numbers before the kill switch can responsibly go on; decide when to resume for Phase 6/7.

**Session summary, 2026-08-29 (new session, `feature/vip-membership-phase6-checkout`, PR #23) — VIP Membership Phase 6 shipped.** Checkout now actually applies member pricing instead of existing as unused scaffolding: `CheckoutController` detects a session-recognized member via `Authorization: Bearer` (silent, never gates guest checkout), `CheckoutService` branches pricing and books real cash margin (`platform_profit = member_price − cost_price`, `reseller_profit = 0` always for member orders — the platform absorbs the whole member discount itself). Insufficient quota silently falls back to standard pricing rather than blocking checkout. Gated on `PlatformSettings.membership_enabled`, matching `CatalogController`'s existing kill-switch gate. Race-safe quota decrement (`MembershipQuotaService`, mirrors `VoucherService::redeem()`'s two-layer lock + idempotency-table pattern — a real double-decrement race was found and closed during build, proven by a two-OS-process concurrency test), a rolling 30-day cycle-reset command (`ResetMembershipCyclesCommand`), `OrderResendService` recomputes member-priced orders via the member formula (live cost, frozen discount%), and the admin order detail surfaces the member badge, tier, member identity, and normal-vs-member price breakdown. Built on top of the already-merged `reseller_cost_price → standard_selling_price` rename (PR #21) and the ADR-027 reconciliation addendum (PR #22). Verified beyond the test suite: a full real-browser checkout through the actual storefront wizard onto a real Xendit sandbox payment page, with the order's pricing/quota/ledger fields confirmed in the DB. Backend 1038/1038 (fast) + 8/8 (concurrency, real MySQL); `admin`/`storefront` `tsc`/`eslint`/`next build` clean.

**Session summary, 2026-08-29 (new session, `feature/membership-fee-registry-money-views`) — VIP Membership Phase 6.5: member registry + fee collection + membership money views.** Founder asked to keep going on membership immediately after Phase 6 shipped. Grilling the "fee collection + money views" gap surfaced it was bigger than it read: **no code path anywhere created a `memberships` row** — Phase 6 only *reads* one at checkout, so the whole feature was inert until a member could exist. Grilled Q1-Q15 (member lifecycle, fee entry, idempotency, report split) and recorded as [ADR-027's 2026-08-29 addendum](./adr.md) — the key settled calls: renewal while active *extends* expiry without touching quota (quota refills only at the 30-day cycle boundary, the stricter anti-abuse reading), fee is admin-typed-with-reason-on-deviation (manual collection reality, VCH Path A precedent), `super_admin`-only, no maker-checker, idempotency-key'd, and the `active → expired` flip became real inside `ResetMembershipCyclesCommand` (nothing had ever written `expired` before). Shipped: `membership_fee_records` table + `MembershipFeeService::recordFeePaid()` (single seam a future payment flow reuses — create/extend/reactivate/plan-change in one transaction, `lockForUpdate` on the membership row, two distinct unique-constraint recoveries for `memberships.email` vs the fee record's `idempotency_key`), `LedgerService::credit()` `$reason` param, `Admin\MembershipController` (`GET /api/memberships`, `POST /api/memberships/record-payment`), `ReportService::membershipBreakdown()` + `GET /api/reports/membership-breakdown`, admin `/admin/membership` "Members" DataTable + Record Payment modal, and a new "Membership" Reports tab. Backend **1054/1054** fast suite + **9/9** concurrency (the new `MembershipFeeRecordConcurrencyTest` proves the fee-records unique index actually serializes two concurrent same-key records — the first attempt deadlocked on a gap-lock because a REPEATABLE-READ snapshot re-read can't see the winner's committed row; fixed with a current `lockForUpdate()` read). `admin/` `tsc`/`eslint`/`next build` clean. Migration applied against the real local dev DB. **Still open:** Phase 7 (WhatsApp renewal reminder), the founder's real fee/quota/discount% numbers, Plunk sender-domain verification, and the `reseller_tier_fee` ledger type (reseller wholesale tiers, still design-only).

**Session summary, 2026-08-29 (new session, `feature/plunk-sender-setup`) — Plunk sender domain provisioned.** Founder asked whether Plunk could be set up while the store's own domain was still TBD; answer (checked against Plunk's live docs) was yes — any domain you control DNS for works, best practice a dedicated sending subdomain. Founder's `fixfastapp.com` (Cloudflare, no existing SPF/MX/DMARC conflict) got `send.fixfastapp.com` added as a Plunk project domain. Cloudflare: added the 3 required SES Easy-DKIM CNAMEs + the optional-but-recommended Custom MAIL FROM pair (MX `plunk.send.fixfastapp.com → feedback-smtp.eu-north-1.amazonses.com` prio 10, TXT `v=spf1 include:amazonses.com ~all`) — all verified authoritative and publicly resolving; pre-existing apex records (A/CNAME, google-site-verification, Cloudflare DMARC, a separate `send.fixfastapp.com` MX/SPF + `resend._domainkey` belonging to another setup) left untouched. `PlunkMailer` now sends the required `from` (`{name: "FixFastApp", email: no-reply@send.fixfastapp.com}`, config/env-driven `PLUNK_FROM_EMAIL`/`PLUNK_FROM_NAME`), `PLUNK_API_KEY` wired in `backend/.env`, `.env.example` updated. Backend 1054/1054 green. **Domain verification in Plunk still `Pending`** — SES's pipeline lags DNS (Plunk's own UI: up to 48h; auto-checks every ~5 min). Confirmed the gate is real: a real `/v1/send` via the actual `PlunkMailer` returns HTTP 403 `Domain "send.fixfastapp.com" is not verified` until it flips to Verified — once it does, re-run the real send test and the browser `/membership` → email-OTP → dashboard flow.

**Design session, 2026-08-30 (`feature/reseller-phase2-grill`) — Reseller feature (Phase 2) grilled end-to-end and recorded as [ADR-056..060](./adr.md). No code — design only.** Founder confirmed a real third-party reseller is onboarding, so Phase 2 moved from speculative to scoped. Six rounds of `/mattpocock-skills:grilling` settled the whole design tree: mental model is **platform : reseller ∷ Gamevion : platform** (the platform is a supplier to its resellers, but also provides them a storefront); **storefront channel only for now** (platform collects full retail into the one company CHIP account, credits the reseller its margin; API/H2H "pull supply" channel with a prepaid wallet is a later ADR); **xenPlatform dropped entirely** — reseller payout is manual bank transfer via the existing WTH-1..5 flow, superseding ADR-001's OWNED-sub-account addendum and ADR-022 decision 2's CHIP-Send path; **`reseller_membership_tiers`** — admin-CRUD paid monthly subscription, wholesale price = `cost_price × (1 + tier.markup_percent)`, falls back to `standard_selling_price` when lapsed (not a shutdown — the reseller just gets the walk-in rate, self-corrects on payment), tier fee debited from earnings (no deposit wallet this phase), `reseller_tier_fee` ledger type + `ChargeResellerTierFeesCommand` + ~3-day grace; **tenant isolation** — ADR-003's promised global scope was never built, ADR-057 builds it (`BelongsToReseller` trait, deny-by-default for the reseller guard) as a hard prerequisite; **separate `reseller/` Next.js app** with its own Sanctum guard + `reseller_users` table (admin-created, set-password invite via Plunk), 7 portal screens, impersonation (RES-4) as a scoped reseller-guard token; **`/admin` Reseller Management** tab (RES-1..6 + tier CRUD + tier-fee recording); **custom domains only, no subdomains** — one `storefront/` app resolves the tenant by `Host` header, TLS via **Cloudflare for SaaS**, this piece blocked on ADR-020 production deploy + ADR-037 staging infra. Five sequenced ADRs, one PR each: 056 (pricing/tiers backend) → 057 (tenant scope) → 058 (auth + admin mgmt) → 059 (`reseller/` portal) → *(prod deploy)* → 060 (branded storefront). Full decision detail + the phasing table + PRD-delta checklist are in ADR-056..060 — not duplicated here.

**ADR-056 built, 2026-08-30 (same session, same branch `feature/adr-056-reseller-tiers`) — reseller wholesale-tier backend core.** All of ADR-056's decisions 1–10 implemented; backend-only, no UI (that's ADR-058), no checkout wiring (ADR-060). Shipped: `reseller_membership_tiers` table + model (admin-CRUD shape, not seeded, not row-count-locked — contrast `membership_plans`); `reseller_subscriptions` table + model (one row per reseller, `active`/`grace`/`lapsed` state) + `ResellerSubscriptionStatus` enum with `grantsWholesaleRate()` (active **and** grace both do — grace is not a downgrade); `PricingService::calculateForReseller()` — wholesale base `= cost_price × (1 + tier.markup_percent/100)` for an active/grace tier, or `standard_selling_price` when `$tierMarkupPct` is null (the lapse fallback — self-corrects on payment, per the founder's own grilled reasoning), then `customer_price = base × (1 + reseller.markup_pct/100)`, `standard_selling_price` still reported as the ORD-9 counterfactual; `LedgerService::debit()` extracted from `withdraw()` with a caller-supplied `$type` (throws `InsufficientBalanceException`, caller decides if fatal), `withdraw()` now a thin wrapper — no behaviour change for existing callers; `ResellerTierFeeService::chargeCycle()` — the single seam that collects one cycle's fee from the reseller's **earnings** balance (no deposit wallet this phase, ADR-056 decision 7), `lockForUpdate()` on the subscription then the `ledger_accounts` mutex inside `debit()`, insufficient → `grace` (3-day `grace_until`, not extended on retry), grace elapsed → `lapsed`, `reseller_tier_fee` ledger type; `ChargeResellerTierFeesCommand` (`app:charge-reseller-tier-fees`, scheduled `daily()` in `routes/console.php`, inert-until-real-cron — same pattern as `ResetMembershipCyclesCommand`). Supersedes `ADR-001`/`ADR-022` for reseller payout was recorded in the ADRs; nothing payout-related is in this backend slice. **16 new fast tests** (`PricingServiceTest` +6, `ResellerTierFeeServiceTest`, `ChargeResellerTierFeesCommandTest`) — backend **1079/1079** fast + **10/10** concurrency (`ResellerTierFeeConcurrencyTest` proves two simultaneous charges debit the fee exactly once — one process sees `active`, the other `grace`). Migrations applied to the local dev DB; FK standalone indexes verified on real MySQL (`reseller_subs_tier_id_index`). Pint clean. **Next in the batch: ADR-057 (tenant isolation), its own branch.**

**ADR-057 built, 2026-08-30 (`feature/adr-057-tenant-isolation`) — tenant isolation mechanism.** ADR-003's promised ORM global scope, never built during MVP, retrofitted before any reseller-guard endpoint exists. Shipped: `App\Support\CurrentReseller` — the request-scoped tenant resolver (`scoped` container binding, reset per HTTP request and per queue job; populated by the ADR-058 reseller-guard middleware, inert everywhere else; tenant id comes from the authenticated `reseller_user`, **never a request parameter**); `App\Models\Scopes\ResellerScope` — not-active → no constraint (admin, console, queue, storefront guest); active + id → `where reseller_id = <id>`; active + no id → `where 1 = 0` (**deny-by-default / fail closed** — a reseller session with an unresolved tenant sees zero rows, never all); `App\Models\Concerns\BelongsToReseller` trait — boots the scope, provides `reseller()` + a `withoutResellerScope()` per-query escape hatch (plus `CurrentReseller::runWithout()` for a block). Trait applied to `Order`, `ResellerBranding`, `ResellerFooterSettings`, `ResellerSeoSettings`, `Redirect` (each model's now-duplicate `reseller()` method removed). `SeoScript` deliberately excluded — its `null reseller_id` means "global script", which an `= <id>` filter would wrongly hide, and it is admin-only with no portal read path. Migration `2026_08_30_110000_add_reseller_id_index_and_backfill_orders`: standalone `orders.reseller_id` index (guarded via `Schema::getIndexes` — SQLite never auto-indexes the FK so the tenant `WHERE` gets its index there; MySQL's `orders_reseller_id_foreign` already covers it) + NULL→`Reseller::platformOwner()` backfill (resolved only when rows actually need it, so a fresh test DB never gets a Reseller row conjured by the migration). **Deviation:** `orders.reseller_id` stays **nullable** — NOT NULL deferred to a follow-up because ~240 existing test fixtures build a bare `Order`; the scope fails closed on reads regardless, so a tenant-less row is invisible under the reseller guard. Follow-up owes an `OrderFactory` then the NOT NULL flip. **6 new tests** (`tests/Feature/Database/ResellerScopeTest.php` — the (a) own-rows / (b) admin-all / (c) queue-all / (d) no-tenant-none matrix across all five models + both escape hatches) — backend **1085/1085** fast + **10/10** concurrency. Migration applied to local dev DB (`orders_reseller_id_index` verified present). Pint clean. **Next in the batch: ADR-058 (reseller auth + admin Reseller Management), its own branch.**

**ADR-058 58a built, 2026-08-30 (`feature/adr-058a-reseller-auth-foundation`) — reseller auth foundation.** ADR-058 split into two PRs (founder call, see the ADR's build addendum): 58a = the backend auth foundation, 58b = admin Reseller Management + UI. Shipped in 58a: `reseller` Sanctum guard + `reseller_users` provider + `reseller_users` password broker (own `reseller_password_reset_tokens` table, 24h expiry — it also backs the set-password invite) in `config/auth.php`; `reseller_users` table (nullable `password` until the invite is accepted; **not** `BelongsToReseller` — only ever queried by email at login, by Sanctum as a token tokenable, or cross-tenant by the admin); `ResellerUser` model; `SetResellerContext` middleware (`reseller.context` alias) — resolves `$user->reseller_id` and calls `CurrentReseller::activate()`, **this is what switches on ADR-057's tenant scope in a real request**, deactivates in a `finally`; `ResellerAuthController` (`login` / `logout` / `me` / `setPassword`) mirroring `AuthController`'s shape (generic credential error, deactivated check, IP-logged failures, null-password short-circuit); `ResellerInviteService::createInviteLink()` (broker token → absolute portal link, `config('services.reseller_portal.url')`); `POST /api/reseller/{login,set-password,logout}` + `GET /api/reseller/me` with own throttle buckets (`reseller-login`, `reseller-set-password` — the ADR-021 shared-bucket lesson). Sanctum v4's `Guard::hasValidProvider` gives the guard separation for free: `auth:reseller` rejects an `admin_users` token; a reseller token on an `admin.role` route is caught by `EnsureAdminRole`'s `instanceof AdminUser`. **MFA for `reseller_users` deferred** (matches the admin MFA gap; WTH maker-checker is the compensating control for portal withdrawals). 20 new tests (`ResellerAuthControllerTest` 12, `SetResellerContextTest` 4, + the auth-separation pair) — backend **1101/1101** fast + **10/10** concurrency. Migrations applied to local dev DB. Pint clean. `RESELLER_PORTAL_URL` still owed in `backend/.env.example` (file not editable this session — default `http://localhost:3002` in `config/services.php`). **Next: ADR-058 58b (admin Reseller Management), its own branch.**

**ADR-058 58b built, 2026-08-30 (`feature/adr-058b-reseller-management`) — admin Reseller Management.** The second half of ADR-058 (58a was the auth foundation): RES-1..6 + the `reseller_membership_tiers` CRUD (ADR-056 decision 1's admin surface) + RES-4 impersonation, all `super_admin`-only on `/admin/resellers`. **Migrations:** `resellers.deleted_at` + `reseller_membership_tiers.deleted_at` (soft-delete — `reseller_subscriptions.reseller_membership_tier_id` is restrict-on-delete so a tier any reseller was ever on can't be hard-deleted); `reseller_tier_changes` (append-only tier-assignment audit, `membership_plan_changes` precedent, both tier FKs nullOnDelete); `reseller_impersonation_sessions` (real admin identity + portal identity + `personal_access_token_id` nullOnDelete + start/end/`ended_reason`). **Backend:** `Admin\ResellerController` (index with batched `LedgerService::balances()` earnings lookup, store = reseller + first `reseller_user` + optional tier + Plunk invite all in one transaction that rolls back on a send failure, update, `assignTier`/`chargeTierFee`/`reactivateSubscription`, `storeUser`/`resendInvite`, `updateStatus`, soft-delete `destroy` guarded on earnings-balance-zero + no pending/approved withdrawal + not-platform-owner); `Admin\ResellerMembershipTierController` (CRUD, delete blocked while an active/grace subscription points at the tier); `Admin\ResellerImpersonationController` (mints `reseller`-guard token via `createToken(name, ['impersonate'], expiresAt)` — Sanctum v4's 3-arg form, 60-min TTL — against the reseller's first invite-accepted active user; audit row; `end` revokes the token; deactivating/deleting a reseller auto-ends live sessions). `ResellerSubscriptionService` (assign/change tier + audit; reactivate) — new seam, billing stays in ADR-056's `ResellerTierFeeService`. `ResellerInviteService::sendInvite()` added (58a only built `createInviteLink()`); `PlunkMailer::send()` extracted as the generic transactional primitive, `sendOtpEmail()` now calls it. **Admin UI:** `admin/src/app/admin/resellers/page.tsx` + `lib/resellers.ts` + 3 modals (`ResellerFormModal`, `ResellerDetailModal`, `ResellerTierFormModal`), PrimeReact-Tailwind throughout (new screen, nothing to migrate — ADR-038); sidebar nav item added. Reseller table (business/tier+status/earnings/orders/domains/status/actions), tier CRUD table, impersonation-session log with an End action. **Deferred to ADR-059/060:** the "impersonating" banner + per-request admin tagging (portal's job); Cloudflare custom-hostname lifecycle on domain add / RES-5 / RES-6. **26 new tests** (`ResellerControllerTest` 16, `ResellerMembershipTierControllerTest` 4, `ResellerImpersonationControllerTest` 6) — backend **1127/1127** fast + **10/10** concurrency, admin `tsc`/`eslint`/`build` clean. Migrations applied to local dev DB. Pint clean. `RESELLER_PORTAL_URL` **still owed** in `backend/.env.example` (file not editable this session — default `http://localhost:3002` in `config/services.php`). `LedgerOwnerType` enum (the 2026-08-30 review's finding 3) still deferred to ADR-059.

**Live-verified same day** (Chrome, real Herd backend + admin dev server, super_admin login by the founder — automated password entry is disallowed in this environment). Walked the full screen: create tier → create reseller (Plunk invite **actually sent** — `send.fixfastapp.com` sender domain is verified now, not still Pending) → `reseller_users` (null password), `reseller_subscriptions` (active), `reseller_tier_changes` (`none → Silver Test`, admin, note) all written → charge fee with zero earnings ⇒ `grace` + `grace_until` +3d, **no ledger entry** → fund earnings via tinker, charge again ⇒ `reseller_tier_fee` −4900 debit, status `active`, cycle advanced → impersonate ⇒ `personal_access_tokens` row with `abilities=["impersonate"]` + 1h expiry + `tokenable=ResellerUser`, audit row → End ⇒ token revoked, `ended_reason=manual` → delete blocked on non-zero earnings, blocked on platform-owner, tier delete blocked while an active subscriber exists → zero the balance, soft-delete succeeds (order history + user rows survive). **Two bugs found and fixed in the same session:** (1) starting an impersonation didn't refresh the on-page sessions list (added an `onRefresh` callback); (2) a soft-deleted reseller's lingering `reseller_subscriptions` row still counted toward a tier's subscriber total and blocked that tier's deletion (`ResellerMembershipTierController` now filters `whereHas('reseller')` in both the count and the delete guard — +1 regression test). All QA data cleaned from the dev DB afterward.

**Next in the batch: ADR-061 (abolish the platform-owner special-case — see next entry), then ADR-059 (`reseller/` portal app).**

**Design session, 2026-08-30 (`feature/adr-061-abolish-platform-owner`) — abolish the platform-owner special-case, recorded as [ADR-061](./adr.md). No code — design only.** Grilled immediately after ADR-058 58b shipped. Trigger: the founder is buying a second business that should behave exactly like the current platform storefront (same catalog, same company wallet, its own domain + branding + consumer membership), while third-party resellers stay feature-limited — and the `Reseller::platformOwner()` discriminator is a magic string (`business_name = 'Platform Owner'`, no unique constraint) that ADR-013's own consequence note already flagged for replacement. **Settled:** every storefront becomes a `Reseller` row, no special path; two new flags — `resellers.is_owned` (our brand; reporting-internal; markup defaults 0 but editable) and `resellers.is_primary` (exactly one row, the fallback tenant for console/jobs/non-`Host` contexts, never deletable) — replace the magic string; `Reseller::platformOwner()` → `Reseller::primary()` across ~28 call sites; consumer membership becomes the sole per-reseller capability toggle (`resellers.membership_enabled`, gated **and** by a retained global `PlatformSettings.membership_enabled` master kill-switch), and it is the founder's chosen "ours vs a reseller's" differentiator; `memberships` + `membership_otp_codes` gain `reseller_id` (per-brand VIP identity — two businesses don't share a roster), `membership_plans` stays global; **`OrderFulfillmentService::creditProfit()` is unchanged** — an `is_owned` brand at markup 0 is already exactly the zero-margin reseller the platform owner is today (the founder's own reframe during grilling is what made this cheap). Two PRs, both before ADR-059: PR-A = the flags + `primary()` + call-site swap + dual kill-switch (pure refactor, no behaviour change); PR-B = `memberships.reseller_id` + an `OrderFactory` + ADR-057's deferred `orders.reseller_id` NOT NULL flip. **Supersedes ADR-013.** Full 9-decision detail + consequence notes + handoffs to ADR-059/060 are in ADR-061 — not duplicated here.

**ADR-061 PR-A built, 2026-08-30 (`feature/adr-061-pr-a-flags`) — flags + `Reseller::primary()`, zero behaviour change.** Migration `2026_08_31_010000` adds `resellers.is_owned` / `is_primary` (nullable + `unique` — the portable "exactly one primary": `1` on the primary, `NULL` on every other reseller, `0` never written) / `membership_enabled`, backfilling the `business_name = 'Platform Owner'` row to `is_owned = is_primary = true` and copying the live global `platform_settings.membership_enabled` onto it. `Reseller::platformOwner()` (magic-string `firstOrCreate`) replaced by `Reseller::primary()` (`where('is_primary', true)->sole()` — fails loud on 0 or >1, never conjures a row) across all 28 non-test call sites; the ADR-057 backfill migration keeps an inline `business_name` lookup since it predates the column. New `Reseller::membershipEnabledEffective()` = the brand flag **AND** the retained global master kill-switch, wired into the 4 Membership gates (`CheckoutController`, `CatalogController`, `MembershipController`). RES-6 delete guard → `is_primary`; RES-2/RES-3 gain an "Our own brand" checkbox (`is_owned`) that reveals the per-brand Membership toggle — the backend forces `membership_enabled` off for a non-`is_owned` reseller and on-editing flushes the catalog cache when markup/membership changes. `TestCase::primaryReseller()` helper + `setUp()` in the 7 endpoint-test classes that relied on the old auto-create; new `tests/Feature/Models/ResellerTest.php`. **9 new tests — backend 1136/1136 fast + 10/10 concurrency**, admin `eslint`/`build` clean, Pint clean. Migration applied to the local dev DB (backfill verified: the primary row carries all three flags). `RESELLER_PORTAL_URL` (+ `ADMIN_URL` / `STOREFRONT_URL`) now documented in `backend/.env.example`. **Next: ADR-061 PR-B** (`memberships.reseller_id`, `OrderFactory`, `orders.reseller_id` NOT NULL, `LedgerOwnerType`), then ADR-059.

**ADR-061 PR-B built, 2026-08-31 (`feature/adr-061-pr-b`) — per-brand consumer-membership identity + `orders.reseller_id` NOT NULL.** Three migrations: `memberships.reseller_id` (nullable → backfill to the `is_primary` row → NOT NULL, FK `cascadeOnDelete`, `unique(email)` → `unique(reseller_id, email)`), `membership_otp_codes.reseller_id` (same, + a `(reseller_id, email)` verify-lookup index, no unique), and ADR-057's deferred `orders.reseller_id` NOT NULL flip — which also had to swap the FK from `nullOnDelete()` (illegal on a NOT NULL column in MySQL, `errno 1830`, and moot under ADR-061 — every order has a real brand, resellers soft-delete) to `restrictOnDelete()`. `OtpService`/`MembershipSessionTokenService`/`CheckoutController`/`CatalogController`/`MembershipController`/`MembershipFeeService` all made brand-aware: the storefront brand resolves via `Reseller::primary()` (ADR-060 → `Host`), the session token embeds + is checked against it, and `Membership` lookups filter `(reseller_id, email)`. A token minted on another brand is silent-guest-fallback at checkout/catalog, 401 at `/membership/me`. `resolve()` now returns `['reseller_id', 'email']` (legacy tokens with no brand claim rejected — membership shipped seeded-off). Admin: `RecordMembershipPaymentRequest` gains a required `reseller_id` (must be `is_owned && membership_enabled`), `GET /api/memberships/brands` for the picker, `?reseller_id=` registry filter, `brand_name` on rows; `RecordPaymentModal` + Members table get a brand select/column (shown only when >1 eligible brand). New `OrderFactory` (repo's 2nd factory; `Order` gains `HasFactory`) — **existing tests not converted** (PR-B plan option i): the ~32 local order-fixture helpers each got one `reseller_id` line; a "consolidate onto `OrderFactory`" pass is a separate follow-up. `LedgerOwnerType` **deferred to ADR-059** (belongs with `ResellerEarningsService`; a ~12-file enum sweep would obscure this PR's diff — the ADR sanctions the deferral). New tests: `MembershipTest` (composite unique), cross-brand OTP/token/checkout/`me` cases, admin brand picker/filter/rejection. **Backend 1146/1146 fast + 10/10 concurrency**, admin `tsc`/`eslint`/`build` clean. **Local-dev-DB incident:** the session ran `php artisan migrate:fresh` (should have been plain `migrate`) — the local sqlite dev DB was wiped and only a bare `db:seed` baseline restored (admin `test@example.com` / `password`, primary reseller, payment methods, hero slide). Any locally-set-up games/packages/test data was lost; staging and production untouched. **Merged to `staging` 2026-08-31 (PR #34). Next: ADR-059** (reseller portal).

**ADR-059 59a built, 2026-08-31 (`feature/adr-059a-reseller-portal-backend`) — reseller-portal read layer + `LedgerOwnerType`.** Three founder decisions taken first (recorded in ADR-059): reseller-portal **MFA descoped** to match admin AUTH-7 (WTH maker-checker still gates payouts); ADR-059 **split into three PRs** (59a backend read layer / 59b `reseller/` app + read screens / 59c storefront-settings + withdrawal + `reseller_game`, each with its own backend so money code ships with its screen); the **Domain screen deferred to ADR-060** (it renders Cloudflare state that doesn't exist yet). Shipped in 59a: `App\Services\Ledger\LedgerOwnerType` (`Platform`/`Reseller`, the ADR-002 literals) + a `coerce()` normalizer — `LedgerService` now takes `LedgerOwnerType|string` (every existing caller unchanged), magic strings swapped at the money seams (`OrderFulfillmentService`, `VoucherService`, `MembershipFeeService`, `ResellerTierFeeService`, `WithdrawalController`, `ResellerController`, `ReportService`, `CustomerAnalyticsService`); `App\Services\Reseller\ResellerEarningsService` — the single seam for every reseller money read (`balance`/`ledgerEntries`/`tierFeeHistory`/`dashboardStats`), filtering `ledger_entries` on `owner_type`/`owner_id` explicitly (no `reseller_id` scope exists there); `GET /api/reseller/{dashboard,orders,orders/{orderNumber},earnings,subscription}` under `auth:reseller` + `reseller.context`, controllers in `App\Http\Controllers\Reseller\`, order responses narrowed like `TrackOrderController` (own margin shown, `cost_price`/`standard_selling_price`/`platform_profit`/supplier/gateway fields never). **21 new tests** (`ResellerEarningsServiceTest`, `ResellerPortalReadTest`, `LedgerOwnerTypeTest`) — cross-tenant isolation, admin-token rejection, financial-field redaction. **Backend 1167/1167 fast + 10/10 concurrency**, Pint clean. No migration (`reseller_game` is 59c). **Local-env note:** the full fast suite now exceeds the founder's local `memory_limit=128M` — `php artisan test` OOMs mid-run (it re-execs PHP, ignoring `-d` on the wrapper); run `php -d memory_limit=1024M vendor/bin/phpunit`. CI unaffected; not a regression (reproduced on clean `staging`). **Next: ADR-059 59b** (`reseller/` Next.js app).

**ADR-059 59b built, 2026-08-31 (`feature/adr-059b-reseller-portal-app`) — the `reseller/` Next.js app + four read-only screens.** Third app alongside `admin/`/`storefront/` — Next 16.3 / React 19 / Tailwind 4 / PrimeReact-Tailwind, `output: standalone`, dev on `:3002` (matches `RESELLER_PORTAL_URL`). Auth/session plumbing mirrors `admin/` exactly (ADR-009): `api-client.ts` verbatim; `session.ts`/`auth.ts` with reseller keys (`kerox_reseller_session`) + `ResellerSessionPayload`; `useClientSession.ts` the same `useSyncExternalStore` shape (dodges the `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` trap, ADR-038 d8); `app/api/{login,logout}` route handlers proxy to `/api/reseller/*` and set/clear the presence-only gate cookie; `proxy.ts` optimistic redirect. `globals.css` + `prime-provider.tsx` + `ThemeContext.tsx` copied from `admin/`. Lean shell (`PortalShell.tsx`) — fixed sidebar (Dashboard/Orders/Earnings/Subscription) + header (business name, theme toggle, sign-out), not the full TailAdmin machinery. Screens: **Dashboard** (earnings/sales stat cards + subscription strip), **Orders** (filterable + paginated → **Order detail** by `order_number`, own margin shown, nothing internal), **Earnings** (withdrawable balance + paginated ledger, credits green / debits red), **Subscription** (tier snapshot + fee charge history). `tsc`/`eslint`/`next build` clean. **No automated FE tests** (neither `admin/` nor `storefront/` has any — ADR-023 E2E golden-paths are the coverage; a portal golden-path waits for a 59c write action). `scripts/dev.sh` now starts `reseller` too (on `:3002`, first run seeds `reseller/.env.local` from `admin/`'s); a `reseller` CI job (tsc/build/lint) added. **CORS fix (same PR):** `config/cors.php` only allowed `ADMIN_URL`+`STOREFRONT_URL`, so every `/api/reseller/*` browser read failed "Could not load" — added `RESELLER_PORTAL_URL` (:3002) + a `CorsConfigTest` guard. **Live-verified in Chrome 2026-08-31** (real Herd backend, seeded test reseller): login → all four screens with real data, delivery-status filter, order detail, dark mode, sign-out + post-logout gate — all work; the CORS gap was the only bug, fixed same PR. **Deferred to 59c:** impersonation banner, all write screens, `reseller_game` catalog toggle. **Deferred to ADR-060:** Domain screen. **Next: ADR-059 59c.**

**ADR-059 59c re-scoped, 2026-08-31 (founder decision).** 59c was "storefront settings + catalog toggle + withdrawal". Re-scoped to **Withdrawal + Profile (bank details) + Impersonation entry & banner** — the parts that work end-to-end before ADR-060. The storefront-config editors (branding text + GA/FB/TikTok pixel IDs, `markup_pct` + live preview, `reseller_game` catalog on/off, Domain screen) **move to ADR-060**, where the per-`Host` branded storefront that consumes them is actually built — today only `Reseller::primary()`'s storefront renders. Founder answers folded in: (a) footer text + legal pages (terms/privacy/about) + footer games stay **admin-only, central** — every brand renders the primary's with `{store_name}` substituted (supersedes part of ADR-028/029's per-reseller-footer intent); (b) SEO — pixel IDs are the only field a reseller controls, rest admin-central; (c) bank details stored on `resellers`, prefill the withdrawal form. Logo upload + per-reseller hero slides + the reseller theme system are all out of 59c/ADR-060-as-currently-scoped — logo/hero are open ADR-060 grilling questions, theme is its own future ADR (PRD §6.15). Full detail in [ADR-059](./adr.md)'s "59c re-scope" note + [ADR-060](./adr.md)'s absorb-addendum.

**ADR-059 59c built, 2026-08-31 (`feature/adr-059c-reseller-withdrawal-impersonation`) — Withdrawal + Profile + Impersonation.** 2 migrations: `resellers` += `bank_name`/`bank_account_no`/`bank_account_holder` (nullable — the reseller's saved payout account, prefills the withdrawal form); `withdrawals.requested_by` → nullable + new `reseller_user_id` FK (a reseller request has no admin requester; `requested_by=null` keeps the admin maker-checker `$admin->id === requested_by` from false-positiving). `Reseller\WithdrawalController` (index/store) — the WTH request side, `owner_type='reseller'`, balance via `ResellerEarningsService`, bank fallback to profile, one-open-request guard, `openAccount()` so the admin approve's `withdraw()->firstOrFail()` has its lock row; **approve/reject/complete stay the unchanged admin flow**. `Reseller\ProfileController` (show/update — contact + bank only; `business_name`/`email` admin-controlled). Impersonation: `/api/reseller/me` returns an `impersonation` block (keyed on an open `ResellerImpersonationSession` for the token id, not token abilities); `POST /api/reseller/impersonation/end` closes it + revokes the token. Portal: **Withdrawal** + **Profile** screens; `app/impersonate` entry (token from URL **hash**, POST to `app/api/impersonate` route handler → verify + gate cookie); `ImpersonationBanner` above every screen when impersonating; nav 4→6; `/impersonate` excluded from proxy matcher. Admin: `ResellerDetailModal` now `window.open('…/impersonate#token=…')` on RES-4 success. **13 new tests** (Profile 3, Withdrawal 6 incl. a portal-request→admin-approve integration, Impersonation 4). **Backend 1182/1182 fast + 10/10 concurrency**, Pint clean; `reseller/`+`admin/` tsc/eslint/build clean. **Live-verified in Chrome** (real Herd backend): withdrawal request → correct `Withdrawal` row + ledger account created + UI guard; profile edit saved (`business_name` untouched); `/impersonate` → banner → Exit → session closed + token revoked + `/login` (no redirect loop). **ADR-059 done — next: production deployment, then ADR-060.**

**Backend architecture health review, 2026-08-30 (post-ADR-057, before ADR-058) — founder-requested.** Full `backend/` sweep + deep look at the reseller seams (`PricingService`, `CheckoutService`, `LedgerService`, `OrderFulfillmentService`, `ResellerTierFeeService`, the ADR-057 scope). **Verdict: healthy** — services are genuine deep modules (`LedgerService` 110 lines, `ResellerTierFeeService` 99, `PricingService` 75), the ADR-056 `debit()` extraction is textbook, folder structure is consistent (`app/Services/<Area>/`), concurrency discipline is uniform, comment density is high but ADR-cited and load-bearing. Largest file is 525 lines (`ReportService`), only one past ~500. Findings, none blocking ADR-058/059, all "shape a seam before ADR-060 stacks on it": **(1)** `CheckoutService::initiate()`'s inline `standard`-vs-`member` pricing ternaries won't scale to the reseller `tier`-vs-`lapsed` branch — extract a `PricingResolution` VO (→ ADR-060 prereq); **(2)** `PricingService::calculateForReseller()` has no production caller yet, only tests — ADR-060 must wire + prove it end-to-end; **(3)** `owner_type` is the string `'reseller'` in code but ADR-057's note said `Reseller::class` — note corrected, `LedgerOwnerType` enum recommended before ADR-059's `ResellerEarningsService`; **(4)** `DB::table('orders')` in `SupplierController` bypasses `ResellerScope` (benign — admin context — but a pattern to guard, already on the ADR-059 grep checklist); **(5)** `app/Support/CurrentReseller` is the lone occupant of `app/Support/` (defensible, it's infra not a service); **(6)** `docs/adr.md` at ~2340 lines is getting hard to navigate — consider an index or an era split, separate work; **(7)** FormRequest coverage 57 files / 98 mutating routes — spot-check for gaps against the "every mutating route" rule. Findings (1)–(4) folded into ADR-057/059/060 consequence notes. No code changed by the review beyond the ADR-057 doc-note fix.

**Storefront rebrand + visual redesign to "PekanGame", 2026-09-01 (`feature/pekangame-storefront-redesign`) — [ADR-062/063/064/065](./adr.md).** Grilled with the founder (`grilling` + `impeccable`). Production deploy is still pending (no server bought), so focus shifted to the storefront: rebrand off "Kedai Runcit Soloz" and replace its one hardcoded dark-green visual world with a **light neo-brutalist "Digital Architect"** world (Space Grotesk + Inter + JetBrains Mono; warm-paper M3 palette — purple primary, cyan secondary, magenta tertiary, poster-yellow warning; 2px ink strokes, hard offset shadows, always-visible focus; two intensity tiers — bold on marketing/catalog, calm on the checkout/money path). **ADR-062** (rebrand): storefront hardcoded fallbacks → "PekanGame"; migration `2026_09_01_010000` renames the primary `resellers.business_name` + `reseller_branding.store_name`; `DatabaseSeeder`/`OrderFactory` literals; `CheckoutService` CHIP payment description → `"PekanGame order {no}"`. Repo/DB/cookie-key identifiers unchanged. **ADR-063**: new `globals.css` `@theme` token world (dual-theme *structure*, light-only v1 — dark toggle deferred to the future per-tenant THM ADR, PRD §6.15), `layout.tsx` fonts, `Logo.tsx` placeholder mark, `Button` rebuilt. Supersedes ADR-028's palette derivation. **ADR-064**: every storefront surface rebuilt — home (hero split-card + Quick Top-Up widget, ProductCard grid w/ branded `PlaceholderTile`, section-heading rhythm), order flow (Stepper, notched StepCard, PackageGrid, sticky OrderSummarySidebar, grouped payment tiles), ReviewModal, order-status tracker, track-order, membership, legal pages, header/footer/announcement/bottom-nav. New `ui/` primitives (`Card`, `Badge`, `PlaceholderTile`, `SectionHeading`); legacy token names fully removed from components + `globals.css`. Hero copy/CTA/image stay admin-driven (`HeroSlideController` untouched). `admin/`/`reseller/` untouched. **`storefront` build + lint green**, `impeccable` `detect.mjs` clean, live-verified in Chrome (home / order page / track-order / footer). Backend fast suite unaffected by the rename (E2E storefront-checkout golden path asserts no brand text). Migration applied to local dev DB.

**Founder feedback folded in, same session:** (1) **`AnnouncementBar` deleted** everywhere (+ component file) — hardcoded marketing copy, not DB-driven, no feature lost. (2) **Quick Top-Up is an icon-tile grid** (up to 6 games — pinned first, padded with top catalog games), not a `<select>`; no "browse all" link. (3) **Nav "Promotions" → "Membership"** (`SiteHeader` + `BottomNav`), **rendered only when `GET /api/membership/plans` is non-empty** (membership effectively enabled — ADR-061 dual gate), so PRD §15's "no membership link until launch" intent is data-enforced, not a manual omission; homepage `PromotionsSection` + `#promotions` anchor stay. (4) **Membership dashboard → Stitch `membership_history` layout** — two-column status card + order-history table (Date / Game·Package / Order# / Price / Status), horizontally scrollable; frontend-only (`created_at` + `order_number` were already in `GET /api/membership/me`); no fake "Account Links" sidebar (ADR-027 "lighter than an account").

**[ADR-065](./adr.md) — order-status detail → Stitch bento.** `GET /api/track-order/{n}` and `OrderStatusUpdated::broadcastWith()` (ADR-047, shared shape) widened via one `TrackOrderController::customerSafePayload()`: **masked** buyer contact (`App\Support\ContactMask` — server-side only, not a model accessor, raw values untouched everywhere else) + a customer-facing payment breakdown (`payment_method` / `selling_price` [the charged price, never `standard_selling_price`] / `voucher_discount` / `transaction_fee`). **Never** `payment_ref` (the Stitch "Transaction ID" row dropped) or any internal financial/supplier field. The existing `throttle:20,1,track-order` is unchanged (~80-bit ULID + rate limit already the control). Storefront renders the bento — Game & Package / Customer Info (masked) / Payment Details + an Amount Paid box. New backend tests: masked shape, raw-value leak guard, broadcast==endpoint parity. Backend **239/239** (Track/Checkout/Membership/Fulfill filter) + **298/299** on the wider Order/Event/Observer net (the one failure — `ResellerEarningsServiceTest::test_dashboard_stats_today_bucket_excludes_older_paid_orders` — is a pre-existing time-bucket flake, reproduces on a clean `git stash`). Pint clean; `storefront` build + lint green; order-status bento live-verified in Chrome with a masked demo order.

**Accepted debt:** one hardcoded visual world until ADR-060's `Host` multi-tenancy + the THM ADR land. Real PekanGame logo asset + hero artwork still owed by the founder (SVG placeholder mark + `PlaceholderTile` in place). **Not merged** — sits on `feature/pekangame-storefront-redesign` until production deploy; **PR #38 to `staging`, all CI green** (admin / backend-tests 1184/1184 / backend-concurrency / playwright 3/3 golden paths / reseller / storefront). Two CI fixes needed, neither from the redesign itself: (a) `ResellerEarningsServiceTest::test_dashboard_stats_today_bucket_excludes_older_paid_orders` was date-fragile (`now()->subDays(3)` crosses into the previous month on the 1st–3rd — reproduces on a clean checkout on 2026-09-01) → anchored with `travelTo(mid-month)`; (b) `OrderStatusTracker`'s delivered stage sub said "Delivered", identical to the delivery `StatusBadge` → Playwright strict `getByText("Delivered")` matched 2 → stage sub renamed to "Complete".

**Rename "kedairuncit(soloz)" → "PekanGame" completed across admin + reseller + the order-number prefix, 2026-09-01 (`feature/rename-pekangame`) — [ADR-062's 2026-09-01 addendum](./adr.md).** Deploy-prep follow-through to ADR-062: the storefront rebrand (ADR-062/063/064) left `admin/` and `reseller/` still showing "KedaiRuncitSoloz", and the customer's bank statement still read `KRS-…`. Since the deploy pipeline is already uniformly `pekangame` (`registry.digitalocean.com/pekangame`, DB `pekangame`, `api.pekangame.space`, `/opt/pekangame`), the founder called it: no two names anywhere a human looks, before go-live. Changed — **`admin/`** `layout.tsx` title / `AppHeader` / `AppSidebar` ("KedaiRuncitSoloz Admin"/"KRS" → "PekanGame Admin"/"PG") + two Settings placeholders; **`reseller/`** `layout.tsx` title; **`README.md`** + root **`AGENTS.md`** H1; **`OrderNumberService`** prefix `KRS-` → `PG-` *and* format `Str::ulid()` (29 chars) → 12 uppercase base36 chars (15 chars, ~62 bits CSPRNG — founder wanted it short, it's on the bank statement; `orders.order_number` `UNIQUE` is the collision backstop; `reference_number`'s `REF-` is brand-neutral, untouched), with `OrderFactory` / `E2ESeeder` + `e2e/tests/constants.ts` (`KRS-E2E-*` → `PG-E2E-*`) / the two prefix assertions / `TrackOrderController` + `OrderStatusUpdated` docblocks / storefront `TrackOrderClient` placeholder all following; **cosmetic:** `BackupRestoreTester` dead MySQL-DB-name fallback + test-fixture backup paths → `pekangame`; brand-string assertions across `Seo`/`Branding`/`Settings`/`Withdrawal`/`Chip`/`Backup` tests. **Deliberately kept** (ADR-062 decision 5 stands): local repo dir, git remote `topup-website`, the real DB name, `kerox_*` cookie prefixes, the Herd dev hostname `kedairuncit-backend.test` (local-only), `.env.example`'s `REVERB_APP_ID`; historical ADR/PRD text and the ADR-020 "kedairuncitsoloz" *partner-company* references left as written. Safe window — production not live, **zero real orders exist**. Backend **1157/1157** (`vendor/bin/phpunit`); `admin`/`reseller`/`storefront` build + lint all green. PR to `staging`.

**Session summary, 2026-09-02 (`feature/adr-066-forge-deploy`) — deploy mechanism switched Docker Compose → Laravel Forge, grilled + recorded as [ADR-066](./adr.md).** Full detail in the §14 entry above and in ADR-066. Grilled the whole Docker-vs-Forge question one round at a time (`/mattpocock-skills:grilling`): root cause (genuine maintenance-surface concern, not just the session's bad luck), maintainability, the scalability concession (ADR-020 decision 4's horizontal-scale rationale, already walked back to vertical by ADR-020's own addendum), Forge plan (**Growth $19/mo** — Hobby's single external-server slot is taken by `nakhoda-prod`; Business adds nothing relevant), provisioning (fresh Forge-owned droplet, retires the SSH blocker), Redis/Horizon/Reverb as native Forge processes (Horizon reads the existing 4-supervisor `config/horizon.php` unchanged), CI (keep every test job, drop only the image pipeline), TLS (Forge-managed Let's Encrypt), and staging (still deferred). **Code shipped this PR:** removed all Docker artifacts (`backend/Dockerfile` + `.dockerignore`, `backend/docker/nginx/`, `docker-compose.prod.yml`, `admin/`+`storefront/` Dockerfiles + `.dockerignore`, `output: "standalone"` from the 3 `next.config.ts`); `ci.yml` drops `build-and-push` + the SSH `deploy` job and gains a `deploy` job (gated on the 5 test jobs) that POSTs `secrets.FORGE_DEPLOY_HOOK`. `backend/docker-compose.yml` (ADR-010 local-dev) untouched. ADR-066 + this §14/§15 entry written. Backend suite / builds not re-run this session (doc + file-removal + one CI-yaml change only). **The dashboard half is founder-driven** — a guided wizard is being produced for: Forge plan upgrade, DO API token, Forge server provision + site + `.env` secrets, Reserved IP re-point, old-droplet destroy, `FORGE_DEPLOY_HOOK` secret, post-cutover cleanup (delete DO Container Registry + stale `DEPLOY_*`/`DIGITALOCEAN_ACCESS_TOKEN` secrets), PHP 8.4 on the box, Reverb `/app/` nginx block, DO monitoring + uptime monitor on `/up`.

**Forge cutover executed, 2026-09-02 — backend + all 3 frontends live in production.** Following [ADR-066](./adr.md). **Backend:** fresh Forge droplet `pekangame-prod` (4 GB / 2 vCPU, SGP1, Ubuntu 24.04 — Forge's first attempt on Ubuntu 26.04 left nginx's binary non-executable; rebuilt on 24.04), Forge server 1247825 on the `JW Brothers` org (Growth $19/mo), site `api.pekangame.space` rooted at `/backend` (monorepo), GitHub branch `main`, Quick Deploy OFF. `.env` entered via the Forge editor (secrets pasted by the founder); `APP_KEY` generated on the box; `QUEUE_CONNECTION=redis` on native Forge Redis; `BROADCAST_CONNECTION=log` (Reverb deferred — storefront's 20s poll fallback covers realtime); `MAIL_MAILER=log`; `MYSQL_ATTR_SSL_CA` skipped for launch (VPC-private + TLS — hardening follow-up). Managed MySQL trusted-source is tag-bound (`topup-prod`) — the droplet needed that tag added before `migrate` could connect, and the `pekangame_app` password needed one reset. Deploy #76703056 (commit `a3c7817`) green: all migrations DONE, `config`/`route`/`event` cached, `storage:link`, `curl /up` → 200. TLS = Forge Let's Encrypt for `api.pekangame.space` only (the `www.api` SAN failed — the Vercel `*` ALIAS is single-level; www-redirect removed from the Forge domain). **Horizon + Scheduler** enabled as native Forge daemons (Horizon "Running", `config/horizon.php` unchanged). `db:seed --class=ProductionSeeder` green (payment methods, crawler rules, primary reseller, branding, super admin). **Frontends:** 3 Vercel projects on `JW Brothers` (Pro) from `farresyh/topup-website` — `pekangame-storefront` (root `storefront`), `pekangame-admin` (`admin`), `pekangame-reseller` (`reseller`), all Next.js 16, all first build green. Env: `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=https://api.pekangame.space` on all three; `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL=https://pekangame.space` on storefront; `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIMEUI_LICENSE_KEY` on admin + reseller. `NEXT_PUBLIC_REVERB_*` deliberately omitted (the echo client is guarded on the key). Domains (DNS already on Vercel nameservers): `pekangame.space` → storefront (apex canonical, `www` → 307 → apex), `admin.pekangame.space` → admin, `reseller.pekangame.space` → reseller — all "Valid Configuration", all `curl` → 200. CORS verified from the storefront origin; `catalog/*` endpoints all 200 (`games`/`payment-methods` return `[]` — no games seeded yet, CHIP FPX still pending — both expected for a fresh prod DB). PHP is 8.4 on the box (Forge default).

**First-boot super-admin gotcha, same day.** After the deploy the founder could not log into `admin.pekangame.space` — "credential incorrect" despite `ADMIN_EMAIL`/`ADMIN_PASSWORD` being set correctly in the Forge `.env`. Root cause: the deploy script runs `php artisan config:cache`, and once `bootstrap/cache/config.php` exists Laravel's `LoadEnvironmentVariables` bootstrapper **skips loading `.env` entirely — for `php artisan` too, not only HTTP** — so `ProductionSeeder::seedFirstSuperAdmin()`'s `env('ADMIN_EMAIL')` / `env('ADMIN_PASSWORD')` both returned `null`, the seeder silently took its "not set — skipping" branch, and `AdminUser::count()` was 0. Fix run via Forge Commands: `php artisan config:clear && php artisan db:seed --class=ProductionSeeder --force && php artisan config:cache` (the seeder is idempotent — `AdminUser::where('email')->exists()` guard). Admin login worked immediately after. **Permanent fix owed (backlog):** `ProductionSeeder` must not read `env()` at runtime — a dedicated `app:create-admin` console command, or a config-backed value. Also folded into [ADR-066](./adr.md)'s cutover addendum.
