**Founder cleanup done post-session (2026-09-02):** ✅ `FORGE_DEPLOY_HOOK` GitHub secret added — the CI `deploy` job now auto-deploys on push to `main`; ✅ old droplet `topup-prod-sgp1` (id 596906796) destroyed; ✅ DO Container Registry `pekangame` deleted. **Still open:** DO Resource Alerts (CPU / memory / disk on `pekangame-prod`) + a DO **Uptime** check on `https://api.pekangame.space/up` (the classifier blocked the session from creating these — founder to do); delete the now-unused `DIGITALOCEAN_ACCESS_TOKEN` GitHub secret. Then CHIP go-live + Digiflazz as before.

**Post-cutover hardening batch, 2026-09-02 (`docs/post-forge-cutover-sync`, PR to `staging`) — the permanent fix for the first-boot super-admin gotcha, plus doc reconciliation.** Bundled onto the same branch as the post-Forge doc sync (not yet on `staging`), one PR. **`ProductionSeeder` env() fix (was pointer item 5):** new `config/admin.php` (`seed_email` / `seed_password` / `seed_name` — env read only inside this config file, so `php artisan config:cache` bakes the value in while `.env` is still loaded) + new `App\Console\Commands\CreateAdminUser` (`app:create-admin`, options `--email` / `--password` / `--name` / `--force`, config fallback, idempotent and deploy-safe: blank config exits 0, an existing account is untouched without `--force`, `--force` resets password + reactivates + re-grants `super_admin`, a bad email/short password exits 1). `ProductionSeeder::seedFirstSuperAdmin()` deleted — the seeder now just `Artisan::call('app:create-admin')`. A reseed after `config:cache` no longer silently skips. 7 new tests (`CreateAdminUserCommandTest`), backend **1164/1164** fast, Pint clean. **Doc reconciliation, same PR:** the `composer.json "^8.3"→"^8.4"` follow-up (pointer item 9 + an ADR-066 consequence note) was itself stale — `composer.json` has declared `"php": "^8.4"` since ADR-020 Phase 1, matching the lock file; both mentions corrected, nothing to change in code. **`MYSQL_ATTR_SSL_CA` (pointer item 7)** stays a founder `.env` task — `config/database.php` already reads it for both the `mysql` and `mariadb` connections; nothing to build.

**Price Sync stuck-run pile-up — real root cause was a missing production `APP_KEY`; code hardened alongside, 2026-09-02 (`fix/price-sync-stuck-runs`, PR #53 to `staging`).** Founder spotted `/middleware/price-sync` in production with ~50 runs stuck "Syncing…" since the cutover. Diagnosis: Horizon running, `APP_ENV=production`, `price-sync` supervisor up — but `php artisan queue:failed` + the `failed_jobs.exception` showed **every** `SyncSupplierPricesJob` failing with `Illuminate\Encryption\MissingAppKeyException: No application encryption key has been specified`, thrown at the job's `Supplier::firstOrCreate(['slug'=>'gamevion'], ['api_config'=>[]])` (line 70) — the `encrypted:array` cast on `api_config` needs `APP_KEY`, and the Horizon worker's resolved config had none. The scheduler fires the run closure every 10 min → each job throws **uncaught** (outside the per-supplier try/catch) → `queue:failed` (`$tries=1`); the job had **no `failed()` method** and there is no reconcile sweep for `PriceSyncRun` the way there is for orders/payments, so each failure stranded its row at `status='running'` forever. **This is an infra gap, not a code bug** — `APP_KEY` was missing/empty in the config the workers boot with (either never written to `.env`, or `config:cache` baked an empty value, or the Horizon daemon was running on a pre-`APP_KEY` config snapshot and was never bounced). It also silently blocked `/admin/suppliers` from ever saving a supplier `api_config` — so it had to be fixed before Digiflazz regardless. Safe to regenerate at the time it was found: **zero encrypted rows existed in the production DB yet** (no supplier config, MFA descoped). **Founder fix on the box:** `php artisan key:generate` (if `.env` truly had none) → `config:clear && config:cache` → `php artisan horizon:terminate` (Forge daemon auto-restarts on the fresh config). **Code hardening shipped in the same PR, as defence-in-depth:** (1) `SyncSupplierPricesJob::failed()` marks the run `failed` with the exception message — a stranded run is always resolvable and its reason visible on `/middleware/price-sync` instead of an eternal "Syncing…"; (2) the `firstOrCreate` stopgap removed — `/admin/suppliers` (ADR-046) is the real path now, the job must not manufacture a supplier; (3) the job filters active suppliers to those with a non-empty `api_config` (an un-configured row is skipped, not attempted-then-failed); (4) the scheduled closure skips creating a run at all when no active supplier has credentials yet (no Sync-History noise pre-launch; the manual "Sync All Prices Now" trigger is deliberately not gated). 3 new tests, backend **1167/1167** fast, Pint clean. **Founder one-time cleanup on the box** after the `APP_KEY` fix + this deploy: `php artisan queue:flush`; `PriceSyncRun::whereIn('status',['queued','running'])->update(['status'=>'failed','finished_at'=>now(),'error_message'=>'orphaned pre-failed()-handler'])`.

**Digiflazz adapter completion — buyer-area scoping + per-supplier Product Manager grouping, 2026-09-02 (`feature/digiflazz-adapter-completion`, PR to `staging`; [ADR-067](./adr.md), grilled via `/mattpocock-skills:grilling`).** Verified live from `admin.pekangame.space` that the Digiflazz **production key works** (`/middleware/request-logs` shows real `api.digiflazz.com/v1/price-list` responses — real SKUs like `axdj1`, not dev test SKUs; the old "still returns dev SKUs" note was stale), the outbound IP is whitelisted (no `rc: 45`), and `checkBalance` returns `deposit: 0` (real prod account, un-funded — founder must top up before a live order). The scheduled sync had **already** pulled Digiflazz into `supplier_products`: Product Manager showed a **`Games` group of 486 items** (every Digiflazz game collapsed into one — its `category` is a flat `"Games"`) plus a junk **`Data` group of 12**. Fix, per ADR-067: **(1)** `SupplierCatalogItem` gains `groupLabel` + `type`; each adapter sets `groupLabel` from its own "which game" field — Digiflazz `"{brand} — {type}"` (its `type` is the region: Umum/Malaysia/Indonesia/Global/…, per the region addendum below), Gamevion `category`. **(2)** new `supplier_products.group_label` (NOT NULL, backfilled `= category_raw`) + `type` columns; `ProductSyncService` writes both. **(3)** Product Manager is now **supplier-aware**: `categories()` groups by `(supplier_id, group_label)` and returns a `supplier` per row, the UI gains a Supplier column + filter, and the link contract changed from `category_raw` to `supplier_id` + `group_label` (`LinkSupplierProductCategoryRequest`, `linkSupplierProductCategory()`, `LinkCategoryModal` — breaking API change, admin ships same PR). Founder can now link Gamevion "Mobile Legends" and Digiflazz "MOBILE LEGENDS" to the same Game; ADR-034's `denomination` dedup picks the storefront winner. **(4)** `category_whitelist` is now a real config field — `SupplierConfigSchema` split into REQUIRED (gates "fully configured") vs OPTIONAL, `category_whitelist` an OPTIONAL `list` type normalised (comma-separated form → trimmed `string[]`, fail-safe to `[]`) on save; digiflazz edit form gains the field (blank = sync every category). `ProductSyncService` also now **prunes** raw un-promoted rows a completed sync no longer returns (whitelist-excluded, or removed from the buyer area) so a stale group stops lingering forever — promoted rows are kept (PackagePriceSyncService still soft-deactivates their Package), and nothing is pruned when the run came back empty. Surfaced as `PriceSyncRun.stats.catalog_pruned`. **(5)** dual product status — an item is `active` only when `buyer_product_status` **and** `seller_product_status` are both true. **(6)** `DigiflazzSmokeTest` now polls `checkStatus` once on a Pending outcome (proving the async path while the webhook stays deferred — polling reconcile covers it). New tests across `SupplierConfigSchemaTest` (7), `DigiflazzAdapterTest`, `GamevionAdapterTest`, `ProductSyncServiceTest`, `SupplierProductControllerTest`, `SupplierControllerTest`; backend **1179/1179** fast, admin `tsc`/lint/build green. Migration run on the local dev DB (plain `migrate`, not `fresh`). **Founder task (not a build blocker):** top up the Digiflazz deposit; optionally set the digiflazz `category_whitelist` to `Games` and/or drop the leftover `Data` SKUs from the buyer area. **Still deferred:** `DigiflazzWebhookController` (ADR-032 decision 4) — its own future ADR.

**Region addendum, 2026-09-02 (`feature/digiflazz-region-grouping`, PR to `staging`).** Founder caught it right after merging ADR-067: grouping Digiflazz by `brand` alone collapses every region of a game (MLBB Malaysia / Indonesia / Global / Umum) into one group. Digiflazz's own buyer area separates them via `type` (the region tabs — a field already in the price-list response and stored since ADR-067). `DigiflazzAdapter` now sets `groupLabel = "{brand} — {type}"` (bare `brand` when no `type`). No schema change — `group_label` just gets a more specific value on the next sync; `updateOrCreate` keys on `external_ref` so existing rows update in place. Matches Gamevion, whose `category` already carries the region.

**Mobile responsiveness pass — storefront + admin/middleware panels, 2026-09-02 (`fix/storefront-mobile-polish` PR #61, `fix/admin-middleware-mobile-nav` PR #60, both to `staging` then `staging`→`main` via PR #62). No grill — pure reskin/fit work off an `/impeccable` audit, live-iterated.** 80% of traffic is mobile and the PekanGame redesign (ADR-062–065) was built desktop-first from the Stitch mockups — made to reflow at breakpoints but not to *fit* a phone. **Storefront (PR #61):** `BottomNav` now pads `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` and `SiteFooter` / per-page `<main>` clear the fixed nav (footer rows were sitting under it); the ~8px horizontal-scroll gutter killed — the checkout `Stepper` and the order-status stage row were `nowrap` flex rows that can't fit a phone, both now render circles + connectors only on mobile (`Stepper` adds a "Step N of M" caption, the status row stacks vertically), full labels return at `lg`; `ReviewModal`'s 8px neo shadow is `lg`-only; `overflow-x: clip` on `<body>` as the net. `HeroSlider`: fixed `h-[360px]` → `min-h` (long headline can't clip), touch-swipe added (arrows pointer-only), 32px title on mobile, `onError` falls back to the no-image paper layout, adds the missing `sizes` prop. Also derives its production `next/image` host from `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` (prod gallery images were 400ing → "jadi hitam"). Plus `loading.tsx` route skeletons for perceived nav latency (catalog *caching* would need its own grill/ADR — stale-price risk on a money path — deliberately not done here). **Admin + Middleware panels (PR #60):** the Middleware Panel had a hand-rolled static sidebar — no mobile drawer (ate half a phone screen), no header, styling that didn't match `/admin`; and the Admin sidebar's own drawer couldn't scroll on mobile (`h-screen` starting 64px below the header pushed Settings off the bottom). New shared **config-driven `PanelShell` + `PanelSidebar`** (link / disabled-placeholder / button / collapsible-group items, multiple sections) — both panels render it so they can't visually diverge again; `AppSidebar` / new `MiddlewareSidebar` are now just nav config. Scroll fixed (`flex-1 min-h-0` nav area). Middleware's Ops items keep their signed-URL new-tab behavior. `admin/next.config.ts` also picked up the `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL`-derived image host in the 2026-09-02 docs-audit-sync pass. Storefront + admin build + lint green; live-verified in Chrome on a phone viewport.

**Docs-audit-sync pass, 2026-09-02 (`fix/docs-audit-sync-2026-09-02`).** Fresh audit of git history vs `prd.md`/`adr.md` found the §15 tracker (its own "live source of truth") lagging: the Supplier Adapter row still called `DigiflazzAdapter` `Http::fake`-only / IP-blocked / no `Supplier` row (contradicted by ADR-067's live-wiring above); the Storefront row said "not yet in production" and framed the prod image-host as an unfixed pre-deploy task; the Resellers row called ADR-060 "blocked on production deploy"; the Price Sync + Middleware Panel rows didn't mention ADR-067's supplier-aware Product Manager; ADR-067's own Context bullet in `adr.md` still said the production key was "unconfirmed". All corrected, plus this §14 entry for PR #60/#61 (the build log had no record of them). One code change bundled: `admin/next.config.ts` given the same `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL`-derived `next/image` host as `storefront/` (it had been left local-hosts-only).

**CHIP webhook model resolved + wired for go-live, 2026-09-02 (`feature/chip-webhook-go-live`, PR to `staging`; [ADR-022](./adr.md)'s 2026-09-02 webhook-model addendum, grilled via `/mattpocock-skills:grilling` 3 rounds since it is the money path).** CHIP FPX is now approved (founder), which unblocked resolving the 2026-09-01 addendum's decision 6 (webhook model deferred to build time). **Decided: per-purchase `success_callback`, not a portal-registered webhook** — CHIP POSTs a signed Purchase (+ `event_type`) to a URL given on `POST /purchases/`, verified against the account key from `GET /public_key/` (exactly what `ChipGateway::verifyWebhookSignature()` + the existing tests already assume). Registered webhooks' typed-event model (`purchase.payment_failure`, `payment.charged_back`, `payout.*`) has no in-scope consumer — refunds are out (ADR-004), FPX/DuitNow QR carry ~zero chargeback, payout is manual (ADR-059). Non-paid outcomes (fail/cancel — `success_callback` never fires for them) are left to `ReconcilePendingPaymentsCommand` (PAY-3, 15-min poll), grilled and accepted (customer already saw the failure + `failure_redirect`; only server-side status/voucher-restore is delayed). **Code:** new `config('services.chip.callback_url')` (default `rtrim(APP_URL,'/').'/api/webhooks/chip'`, env `CHIP_CALLBACK_URL` override for a local tunnel); `ChipGateway` constructor arg `callbackUrl` + `success_callback` in the `createPayment()` payload; `verifyWebhookSignature()` now re-fetches the public key once and retries if a cached key stops verifying (CHIP-side rotation), rate-limited to 1 `GET /public_key/` per 60 s against a forged-signature flood; route named `webhooks.chip`; `ChipWebhookController` logs `event_type` (informational — keys off `status`). 4 new/changed tests in `ChipGatewayTest` (`success_callback` sent / omitted, key-rotation re-fetch + retry, refetch rate-limit); `ChipGatewayTest`+`ChipWebhookControllerTest` **25/25**, Checkout/Payment/Reconcile/Fulfillment **197/197**, Pint clean. **Not done (go-live sequence, next):** (a) local backend + `cloudflared` tunnel + CHIP test key → real signed callback verified end-to-end (delivery via Gamevion sandbox, zero money); (b) prod + CHIP live key + one real RM1 FPX (delivery may land `NeedsReview`, cleared by hand); (c) activate the `fpx` `payment_methods` row; (d) DuitNow QR as a separate phase (same callback, but its QR render model still needs the check ADR-022's 2026-09-01 decision 5 describes). **Founder task:** put the **live** `CHIP_SECRET_KEY`/`CHIP_BRAND_ID` in the Forge `.env` (the smoke test used a test-mode key).

**Go-live step (a) tooling, 2026-09-02 (`feature/chip-webhook-smoke-test`, PR to `staging`).** Two things so step (a) is a one-command run, not a fragile hand-procedure: **`app:chip-webhook-smoke-test`** (`app/Console/Commands/Smoke/`, joins `app:chip-smoke-test`/`app:gamevion-smoke-test`) — `createPayment` (real, `success_callback` in the payload) → `POST /purchases/{id}/mark_as_paid/` → polls the real Order for the signed callback → prints a verdict; creates one real Order against the cheapest active supplier-linked Package (`--package=<id>` to pick, `--cleanup` to delete after), refuses `APP_ENV=production` and a non-public `CHIP_CALLBACK_URL` (either would orphan a CHIP purchase). 4 guard-only tests (the happy path hits the real CHIP API, like every Smoke/ command). And **`scripts/chip-webhook-tunnel-test.sh`** — a `/wizard`-style guided runner: preflight (cloudflared/php/docker, Redis up, CHIP key present) → `cloudflared` quick tunnel to the Herd backend (`--http-host-header` so Herd routes the vhost) → upserts `CHIP_CALLBACK_URL` into `backend/.env` → backgrounds a `queue:work` → runs the smoke command → **reverts `CHIP_CALLBACK_URL` and kills the tunnel/worker on exit (EXIT trap, survives Ctrl-C)**. Verified `mark_as_paid` is a real CHIP Collect endpoint and test-mode purchases can be marked paid this way (CHIP OpenAPI spec + docs).

**CHIP go-live executed — the platform took its first real money, 2026-09-02→03.** **(a)** `scripts/chip-webhook-tunnel-test.sh` run against the real CHIP API (test key + `cloudflared` tunnel): a signed `success_callback` was verified (real `GET /public_key/`), parsed, and flipped a real Order to `Paid` — the webhook half, unverified since the CHIP-only cutover, is now proven. **(b)** the founder made a **real RM1.94 FPX payment on production** with the **live** CHIP key — money genuinely debited from the founder's own bank account (`pekangame.space`, order `PG-PYAYMRYNUYV0`, member checkout: Tier 2 / 80% off / member `faris9745@gmail.com`, selling RM0.94 + RM1.00 FPX flat fee = RM1.94): CHIP hosted page → real bank → `success_callback` to `https://api.pekangame.space/api/webhooks/chip` → signature verified → `payment_status = Paid` → `FulfillOrderJob`. The `fpx` `payment_methods` row is **active** in production (fee RM1.00 flat). **CHIP is proven end-to-end in production, with real money.**

**What happened to the money:** `/admin/reports` shows **Total Sales RM1.94, Owner Profit RM0.00** — exactly right. Sales counts paid orders; profit is ledger-sourced (`ledger_entries` type `order_profit`) and `OrderFulfillmentService` writes that credit **only on a delivered order**. This order's **delivery `failed`** (`rc 44` "Saldo tidak cukup" — Digiflazz deposit is Rp 0), so **no `order_profit` ledger entry exists** — `Order.platform_profit` (RM0.02, ORD-9-stamped at checkout) is an estimate, not a booked credit. The RM1.94 is real revenue held by CHIP pending settlement to the company bank; the RM0.02 margin books to the ledger only once the order is delivered (top up Digiflazz → Resend Delivery) or is written off as a voucher (ADR-004).

**Known minor quirk:** `/middleware/payment-methods` "Test This Channel" on `fpx` shows `failed: [400] CHIP request failed with HTTP 400` — the `PaymentMethodController::test()` probe sends a nominal payload CHIP rejects, even though a real checkout with full customer data works (proven by `PG-PYAYMRYNUYV0`). Same class as the Xendit-era `test()` finding. Follow-up: send a fuller probe payload, or drop the action — not launch-blocking.

**Remaining for commercial launch:** fund a supplier (both Digiflazz and Gamevion are Rp 0) and promote real catalogue. Payment, webhook, and `fpx` are all live. DuitNow QR + FPX B2B1 stay inactive (later phases).

**Digiflazz — HTTP 4xx business-envelope fix, 2026-09-03 (`fix/digiflazz-4xx-business-envelope`, PR to `staging`; [ADR-030](./adr.md)'s 2026-09-03 addendum, grilled 1 round).** `PG-PYAYMRYNUYV0`'s delivery failure surfaced as a bare `"Digiflazz request failed with HTTP 400"` in `/admin/orders` while `/middleware/request-logs` (ADR-051) showed the real body — `{"data":{"rc":"44","status":"Gagal","message":"Saldo tidak cukup"}}`. Digiflazz wraps an ordinary business rejection in an **HTTP 400**, contradicting this adapter's original "always HTTP 200 for a well-formed request" assumption. `DigiflazzAdapter::submitTransaction()` / `failureFrom()` checked `$response->failed()` first and discarded `rc`/`status`/`message`. **Fix:** a real **5xx** is always a transport error (`isServerError: true`, breaker-counting); for a **2xx or 4xx** the response body is authoritative — `data.status` → the existing Sukses/Pending/Gagal `match`, a bare `data.rc` → a Failure with that `rc`/`message`, no usable body → the generic `"HTTP {status}"`. A business `Gagal` wrapped in a 4xx **never** sets `isServerError` (never trips `CircuitBreakingSupplierAdapter` — matches `SupplierResponse::failure()`'s documented intent). Both Digiflazz paths (`submitTransaction` incl. `checkStatus` re-submit, and `failureFrom` for `checkBalance`/`listProducts`); `GamevionAdapter` left alone (already special-cases 409/422, no evidence it's lossy — noted in the ADR for a separate look). No `rc`-code lookup table — Digiflazz sends a human `message` every time. 6 new `DigiflazzAdapterTest` cases; Digiflazz/CircuitBreaker/OrderFulfillment/Reconcile suites **82/82**, Pint clean.

Items 4, 6, 9, 10 carry forward unchanged.

(The storefront-checkout Playwright flake was the default 30 s per-test timeout being too tight for a ~10-step flow whose last step is `next dev`'s cold-compile of the status route — fixed by raising `e2e/playwright.config.ts` `timeout` to 60 s, PR #50.)

**Session summary, 2026-09-03 (`feature/adr-068-self-serve-membership-subscription`) — VIP Membership Phase 7 (payment half) designed + grilled as [ADR-068](./adr.md); PR-0 (member-identity fix) shipped.** The founder chose to strengthen Membership before resuming the reseller backlog, starting with self-serve subscribe+pay (a verified member pays for a tier themselves on `/membership` through the existing CHIP checkout; the webhook creates/extends the membership via the same `MembershipFeeService::recordFeePaid()` seam the admin Record Payment action uses). Grilled to 17 settled decisions over three rounds, then stress-test re-grilled (16 findings) — full record in ADR-068 + its stress-test addendum. Key shape: a new `membership_checkout_attempts` table holds `pending → paid/failed/expired` state; one webhook endpoint branches by which table owns the reference; the webhook matches on a server-generated `subscription_number` (`MS-xxx`) known before CHIP is called (closing the lost-response gap); amount server-computed + snapshotted (`fee_sen` + `total_charged_sen`, channel fee on top, ledger books `fee_sen`); `recordFeePaid` `$adminUserId` → `?int` for the system path; the receipt email moves into the seam so admin + self-serve both send one. Build sequencing: PR-0 (member-identity fix), PR-1 (money-path core + concurrency/e2e tests), PR-2 (storefront subscribe surface), PR-3 (admin `/admin/membership/[id]` detail). **PR-0 shipped 2026-09-03 (PR #69), surfaced by order `PG-PYAYMRYNUYV0`:** the member was priced correctly at the member rate but had mistyped the checkout contact email, so the order never showed in their `/membership` history (matched strictly on `customer_email`). `CheckoutService::initiate()` now forces `customer_email` to the membership's OTP-verified email whenever a member session is present (from the membership id, so an out-of-quota standard-fallback order still gets it — name/phone stay as typed); `GET /api/membership/me` returns that email and the storefront pre-fills + locks the checkout email field for a signed-in member; `MembershipController::orderHistory()` now matches `customer_email OR membership_id` so an order bought as this member surfaces regardless of its contact email. Bundled a fix for the `storefront-checkout.spec.ts` "Delivered" e2e flake.

**PR-1 shipped 2026-09-03 (`feature/adr-068-pr-1-subscription-payment`) — the money-path core.** `membership_checkout_attempts` table (`pending → paid/failed/expired`; three identifiers, two amounts, stored `checkout_url`) + `MembershipSubscriptionService` (`initiate()`: dedup on the client key and a 15-min pending window, server-snapshotted `fee_sen` + channel fee, one synchronous `createPayment`, `failed` on rejection, a same-`subscription_number` resume for a lost response; `completePaidAttempt()`: the one seam the webhook + reconcile both call). New endpoints `POST /api/membership/subscribe` (throttled, kill-switch gated) and `GET /api/membership/subscribe-options` (full plans + `current_plan_id` + `relation`; an `expired` member reads as no current plan). `ChipWebhookController` gains a membership branch after the order lookup, matched on `subscription_number` (the event `referenceId`), amount cross-checked against `total_charged_sen`. `MembershipFeeService::recordFeePaid` `$adminUserId` → `?int`; the system path derives its own `reason`; `SendMembershipReceiptJob` (queued `PlunkMailer`) now fires on every genuine create/transition — admin `record-payment` and self-serve alike (RM0 renders as "Complimentary membership"). `app:reconcile-pending-membership-payments` scheduled `daily()`. Tests: `MembershipSubscriptionControllerTest` (10), webhook branch cases in `ChipWebhookControllerTest`, `ReconcilePendingMembershipPaymentsCommandTest` (6), `MembershipCheckoutWebhookReconcileConcurrencyTest` (the S14 two-process race), and `e2e/tests/membership-subscribe.spec.ts` (verify → subscribe → fake-pay → active; `E2ESeeder` plants a pre-verifiable OTP fixture + turns membership on). Backend **1220/1220** fast + **11/11** concurrency; migration applied to the local dev DB.

**PR-2 shipped 2026-09-03 (`feature/adr-068-pr-2-storefront-subscribe`) — the storefront subscribe surface.** `/membership`'s "coming soon" placeholder is replaced by `MembershipSubscribe` — a tier card per plan (fee/month, quota, `Save X%`), CTA labelled `Subscribe` / `Renew` / `Upgrade` from `subscribe-options`, a `downgrade` tier disabled with a note (S5). Pick a tier → payment-method radios (`listPaymentChannels()`, auto-selected when there's one) → `Continue to Payment` → redirect to the CHIP `checkout_url`. `MembershipClient` reads `?checkout=success|failed`: `success` polls `GET /api/membership/me` every 3s for ~30s then shows the active card (or a "check your email" fallback — the receipt is the backstop), `failed` shows a "nothing was charged" banner. `lib/membership.ts` gains `getSubscribeOptions()` / `subscribe()`; the page is wrapped in `<Suspense>` for `useSearchParams`. `e2e/tests/membership-subscribe.spec.ts` upgraded to drive the real UI; full e2e suite green (5/5); storefront `tsc`/`lint`/`build` clean. A founder review of the preview then reshaped the no-membership state — a "You're not a member yet" orientation card above the grid, Tier 2 as a visual hero (cyan fill + a floating yellow "BEST VALUE" ribbon + filled CTA) so the eye anchors on it, equal-height cards. Fixed a latent dead `gap-gutter` class.

**PR-3 shipped 2026-09-03 (`feature/adr-068-pr-3-admin-member-detail`) — the admin per-member detail.** `GET /api/admin/memberships/{id}` returns state + fee-payment history (each linked to its `ledger_entries` row via a new `membership_fee_records.ledger_entry_id`) + the last 25 `membership_checkout_attempts` for the member's email **including pending / failed / expired** (so support can answer "I paid but I'm not active") + a member-orders summary. New route `/admin/membership/[id]` (mirrors `customer-analytics/[email]`), reached via a "View" button on the Members registry; `page.tsx` (519 lines) split into `TierCard` + `MembersSection` components. Backend 1223/1223; admin tsc/lint/build clean.

**ADR-068's payment half (PR-0..3) is complete.**

**Session, 2026-09-03 — storefront perceived-performance + mobile buy-flow designed + grilled as [ADR-071](./adr.md); PR0 built.** The founder wanted the customer purchase flow (land → pick package → pay → get diamonds) to feel smooth on mobile, where ~80% of buyers are, and asked whether to optimise UI or backend first. A live audit (production, 390px viewport via a same-origin iframe harness) + a code read confirmed the causes are all perceived-latency and layout, not server throughput: **zero `loading.tsx`** anywhere (tap → 1–2s frozen screen — measured `/` → `/order/[slug]` = 1867ms, revisit 2082ms); **`layout.tsx` + every page `force-dynamic`** killing prefetch + the Data Cache app-wide; **`apiFetch` bare `fetch` with no cache directives** so every SSR render round-trips to Forge; **the `/order/[slug]` page awaits four data calls in series** (~1.6s RSC); **`listPlans()` fetched twice per nav** (server prop + `BottomNav` client effect); **a logged-in member triggers a second client data wave** (`localStorage` token unreadable in SSR); **`ReviewModal` shares `z-50` with `BottomNav`** so the fixed nav paints over the "Confirm & Pay" button, and the modal has no `role="dialog"` / focus trap / scroll lock / Esc; **`<main pb-10>` < the ~60px nav** clipping the last footer line and card bottoms; **the Order Summary card is `lg:sticky` only**, sitting after 60+ packages on mobile. Grilled with the founder over four rounds → 14 decisions, delivered as four PRs off `staging`: **PR0** content fixes; **PR1** perceived latency + layout + a11y + `/order/status` state machine + adaptive poll + payment overlay (no backend); **PR2** data layer (remove `force-dynamic` via graceful `lib/*` fallbacks, `next: { tags: ['catalog'] }` + a `POST /api/revalidate` webhook fired next to every backend `forgetCache()`, parallelize the RSC waterfall, kill the duplicate `listPlans`, migrate the membership token to a cookie — ADR-027 addendum); **PR3** Web Vitals RUM + finishing the deferred ADR-047 Reverb deploy + deploy-time backend caches. Performance budget agreed: feedback < 100ms, INP < 200ms, warm nav < 500ms, cold nav < 1200ms — the founder's initial "every page < 100ms" reframed as a feedback budget, not a content-settle one. **PR0 built (`feature/adr-071-pr0-content-fixes`):** `BottomNav`'s Support link now resolves from admin Store Branding (`social_links.whatsapp`, else a `wa.me/` link from `support_phone` — new `lib/whatsapp.ts` helper, shared with `SiteFooter`), dropping the hardcoded `wa.me/60000000000`; the "This Week's Promotions" section + its `PROMOTIONS` placeholder array (past "Ends: Jul 2026" dates, live in production) removed entirely — real promotions need an admin-editable model, its own future ADR; the hero scrim strengthened for mobile (a bottom-anchored vertical gradient — the old left→right one left the right half of the bottom-anchored headline over an undimmed image at phone width; `lg:` keeps the left-anchored gradient); `SeoBlurb`'s dead "Read More" `href="#"` pointed at `/about-us`. **Founder task after deploy:** set the real Support WhatsApp number in `/admin/settings` → Store Branding (given: `+65 8275 1992`).

**PR1 built + Vercel-preview-verified 2026-09-03 (`feature/adr-071-pr1-loading-and-caching`, PR #80 → staging).** The original PR1/PR2 split collapsed into one PR — a `loading.tsx` fallback can't reveal while the root layout blocks on an uncached `await`, so the skeletons need the caching rework in the same change (ADR-071 §Delivery updated). **Perceived latency:** route-scoped `loading.tsx` matched-layout skeletons for `/order/[slug]`, `/order/status/[orderNumber]`, `/track-order` (`Skeleton` primitive + `.neo-skeleton` sweep, reduced-motion aware); measured on the preview deploy — **tap → skeleton ~115ms**, content swaps in cleanly. No root `app/loading.tsx` (a root-level one never revealed on a streamed load locally — a local-only TLS artifact, but route-scoped works everywhere and `/` is static/ISR anyway). **Caching:** `force-dynamic` off the root layout + `/`, `/about-us`, `/terms`, `/privacy`, `/membership`, `/track-order` (kept on the `robots`/`sitemap`/`llms.txt` route handlers). Catalog/SEO/branding/hero/payment/plans reads go through Next's Data Cache (`storefront/src/lib/cache.ts` `catalogCache` — 60s interim TTL + `catalog` tag; PR2 wires the purge webhook). Every read wrapped in `safeRead` → empty catalog / default branding / clean 404, never a 500; `next build` now prerenders the static routes with no reachable API. `getGame`/`getGamePackages` tolerate a 404 (game with no packages → "no packages yet"; unknown slug → static `app/not-found.tsx`). **Waterfall:** `/order/[slug]` RSC is one `Promise.all` of 5 reads, not 4 serial awaits. **Dup fetches:** new `SiteConfigProvider` in the layout carries `membershipEnabled` + `branding` server-side — `SiteHeader`/`BottomNav` drop their per-nav `listPlans()`/`getBranding()` `useEffect`s; `OrderStatusTracker`'s leftover hardcoded `wa.me/60000000000` uses the resolved support href. **Layout:** persistent chrome (`SiteHeader`/`BottomNav`) moved into `layout.tsx`; new `pb-nav` utility (`calc(4.5rem + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))`) on every `<main>` + `SiteFooter` fixes the clipped last footer line. `tsc`/`eslint`/`next build` green.

**PR2 built + CI-green 2026-09-03 (`feature/adr-071-pr2-revalidate-webhook`, PR #81 → staging).** Decision 3 (revalidation webhook) + the memo/debounce half. **Webhook:** storefront `POST /api/revalidate` (shared-secret `X-Revalidate-Secret` header, fail-closed, `revalidateTag('catalog','max')` — SWR, since `updateTag`'s blocking revalidate is Server-Action-only); backend `App\Services\Cache\NextRevalidation::purge()` at the 7 `forget*Cache()` choke points (Catalog ×3, Branding, Seo, HeroSlide, PaymentMethodCatalog — every mutation path funnels through one), dispatched as a queued `ShouldBeUnique` (10s) `PurgeNextCatalogCache` job so a price-sync burst = one POST; 3 tries then log-and-stop; `config/services.php` `next` block; 8 new tests. **Perf:** `React.memo` on `PackageGrid` + `OrderSummarySidebar`, `useCallback` on `openReview`, 150ms `previewCheckoutTotal` debounce (INP < 200ms on a package tap). **Membership-token `localStorage`→cookie (decision 6) was built here then reverted** — a browser-automation probe reported the `/order/[slug]` `loading.tsx` fallback as stuck after adding `await cookies()` to the page; a screenshot later showed the page actually renders fine (the probe was reading a stale DOM), but by then the revert was pushed. Deferred to **PR2b**, to be redone with the `cookies()` read in a nested `<Suspense>` child and screenshot-based verification. **Founder deploy task:** `NEXT_REVALIDATE_URL` + `NEXT_REVALIDATE_SECRET` on Forge; `REVALIDATE_SECRET` (same value) on the storefront Vercel project.

**PR3 built + CI-green 2026-09-03 (`feature/adr-071-pr3-checkout-surface`, PR #82 → staging).** Checkout/status surface (decisions 7 remainder, 8, 9, 11). **ReviewModal:** `role="dialog"` + `aria-modal` + focus trap + Esc + body scroll lock + `z-[60]`; the "Confirm & Pay" button moved into a **sticky footer inside the sheet** — no longer runs off-screen behind the bottom nav. **Sticky mobile "Review & Pay" bar** on `/order/[slug]` (always-visible Total + CTA) that replaces `BottomNav` on that route (`BottomNav` returns `null` on `/order/<slug>`, not `/order/status`); desktop `OrderSummarySidebar` is `lg`-only, the promo card still shows on mobile. **`PackageGrid`:** "Show all N packages" collapse (first 12; selected stays visible) — real category tabs need a catalog group field that doesn't exist, name-parsing is per-game fragile, and the collapse + sticky CTA already kill the scroll marathon. **`/order/status`:** animated "Delivered" banner (CSS pop-in + self-drawing checkmark, reduced-motion aware) + **adaptive fallback poll** (2s first minute → 10s → 20s, ~6 min total) so "delivered" lands ≤2s while Reverb stays deferred. **Checkout submit:** blocking "Taking you to payment…" overlay (`z-[70]`) over the redirect gap. View Transitions stay deferred (React 19.2 here has no `ViewTransition` export) — a later delight pass. `tsc`/`eslint`/`next build` green.

**PR2b built + preview-screenshot-verified 2026-09-03 (`feature/adr-071-pr2b-membership-cookie`, PR #83 → staging).** The membership-token `localStorage`→cookie migration (decision 6, ADR-027 addendum), redone after the PR2 revert. `lib/membership-session.ts` → `SameSite=Lax` cookie; `lib/membership-session-server.ts` → `getServerMembershipToken()`. New **`MemberAwareOrderForm`** server component reads the cookie and resolves a verified member's personalized packages + email server-side, wrapped in `<Suspense fallback={<OrderFormSkeleton/>}>` in the page — the page body never `await`s a dynamic API so the route `loading.tsx` is structurally safe. `OrderForm` seeds state from the SSR props; its client `getGamePackages(token)`+`getMe(token)` effect now fires only on a sign-in/out while on the page. Guest flow unchanged, `cookies()` resolves instantly for them. `useMembershipToken` dropped the dead cross-tab `storage` listener. `tsc`/`eslint`/`next build` green; guest checkout + skeleton reveal screenshot-verified on the preview.

**PR4 built + CI-green 2026-09-03 (`feature/adr-071-pr4-observability-reverb`, PR #84 → staging).** Observability half of decisions 10/12/14. **Code:** `@vercel/speed-insights` (`<SpeedInsights/>` in `layout.tsx`) for the standard Core Web Vitals dashboard; new `WebVitals` client component (`useReportWebVitals` → `navigator.sendBeacon('/api/vitals')`) mirrors every CWV measurement to a `[web-vitals] <name> <value> <rating> <path>` log line, since Speed Insights' free tier samples; `storefront/src/app/api/vitals/route.ts` is a `force-dynamic` handler that logs and returns 204 — no DB table, the dashboard is the analysis surface. `tsc`/`eslint`/`next build` green (`/api/vitals` builds as `ƒ`). **Infra half is a founder task — no Forge/Vercel/Cloudflare dashboard access in-session** — delivered as an 11-stage `/wizard` runbook (kept in the session scratchpad, `adr-071-deploy-wizard.sh`, not committed per the wizard one-run convention): generate `NEXT_REVALIDATE_SECRET` + Reverb keys; Forge `.env` (revalidate URL/secret + `BROADCAST_CONNECTION=reverb` + `REVERB_*`); publish Reverb config; Forge Reverb daemon (`reverb:start --host=0.0.0.0 --port=8080`); nginx `/app` WebSocket-upgrade `location` block; deploy-script caches (`config:cache`/`route:cache`/`view:cache`/`event:cache`, `--optimize-autoloader`); `config:cache` + Horizon/Reverb bounce; Vercel storefront env (`REVALIDATE_SECRET` + `NEXT_PUBLIC_REVERB_*`); Vercel admin env (`NEXT_PUBLIC_REVERB_*` — reseller has no Reverb dep); Cloudflare WebSocket check; end-to-end verify. Until the founder runs it: storefront keeps the PR3 adaptive poll (functional, ≤2s lag), catalog staleness falls back to the 60s TTL. **The Cloudflare API edge-cache eval (decision 14) stays a follow-up**, gated on PR4's RUM showing whether warm-nav LCP/INP still misses.

**ADR-071 (PR0–4) + ADR-069 (PR-1..3) released to production 2026-09-03 — `staging`→`main` PR #85, merge commit.** CI `deploy` job green (Forge hook POSTed); `api.pekangame.space/up` + `pekangame.space/` both 200 after deploy. The storefront/admin/reseller Vercel projects auto-deployed from `main`. **Not yet verified on the box:** that `migrate --force` in the Forge deploy script applied ADR-069's additive migrations (`supplier_products.raw_price`/`raw_currency`, `suppliers.low_balance_threshold`/`webhook_secret`) — founder to confirm with `php artisan migrate:status`.

**ADR-071 PR4 infra + ADR-069 webhook fully deployed & verified in production 2026-09-04** (founder ran the 11-stage `/wizard` runbook with in-browser assist). Confirmed live:
- **Migrations** — `migrate:status` clean; `raw_price`/`raw_currency` = Ran. `low_balance_threshold` + `webhook_secret` are `Supplier.api_config` keys (encrypted JSON), never columns — no migration for them, and none pending.
- **Reverb** — Forge "Background process" `php8.4 artisan reverb:start --host=0.0.0.0 --port=8080` (dir `/home/forge/api.pekangame.space/backend`) = Running. nginx `location /app { proxy_pass 127.0.0.1:8080; …Upgrade… }` added to the General site config inside `server{}` before the FORGE CONFIG include (one line, prefix `/app` also catches `/apps/*` — Reverb's HTTP events API). `api.pekangame.space/app` → Reverb's own `404 "Not found."` (healthy — keyless path); `/apps/1/channels` → Reverb `"No matching application for ID [1]"` (proxy + daemon proven). `BROADCAST_CONNECTION=reverb` + `REVERB_*` in Forge `.env`.
- **Revalidate webhook** — `NEXT_REVALIDATE_URL`/`_SECRET` in Forge `.env`; `REVALIDATE_SECRET` (Sensitive, non-public) on Vercel storefront; `NEXT_PUBLIC_REVERB_*` on Vercel storefront + admin (app *key* is a public Pusher-protocol identifier; the *secret* stays Forge-only). Both Vercel projects redeployed (no build cache, so `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` re-inlined).
- **Deploy script** — already had `--optimize-autoloader` + `config:cache`/`route:cache`/`event:cache` + `horizon:terminate` + `/up` health curl; `view:cache` skipped (API, minimal Blade). No change needed.
- **Cloudflare** — N/A: `api.pekangame.space` → DO droplet direct (`137.184.250.200`, no `cf-ray`), `pekangame.space` → Vercel. Nothing proxied through Cloudflare yet (that's ADR-060's future cutover).
- **End-to-end** — founder confirmed: order status page flips to Delivered ~instantly (Reverb push, not the 20s poll); admin Price Sync updates live; a package price edit shows on the storefront in ~2s (webhook revalidate), not 60s.
- **Digiflazz webhook** — Payload URL `https://api.pekangame.space/api/webhooks/digiflazz` + Secret registered in the Digiflazz panel, matching `Supplier.api_config['webhook_secret']`. Signature is HMAC-SHA1 of the raw body → `X-Hub-Signature: sha1=…`. Will prove itself on the first real `rc 03` pending order.

**Still open (founder, not blocking):**
- Forge site → Laravel panel still shows "Reverb — Enable" (unclicked): leave it — clicking would try to auto-create a *second* daemon + nginx block. The manual setup is the live one. **Founder confirms will not click it.**
- `DIGITALOCEAN_ACCESS_TOKEN` GitHub secret: founder has decided to leave it in place, not delete — no longer a to-do.

**Catalogue + Membership numbers — founder confirms done, 2026-09-04:** several games are now fully set up in the real catalogue (beyond the single MLBB placeholder this doc previously tracked), and real Membership tier fee/quota/discount numbers are set in `/admin/membership` (no longer the seeded drafts). **Supplier funding (Digiflazz + Gamevion, both Rp 0) is a deliberate hold, not an oversight** — founder is not funding a supplier yet. Until funded, paid orders still can't deliver, so this remains the actual gate on taking real commercial orders — just not an action item for the next session to chase.

Native View Transitions (decision 8) stay deferred — React 19.2 in this repo has no `ViewTransition` export. Cloudflare API edge-cache (decision 14) is RUM-gated.

**➡️ NEXT SESSION STARTS HERE (2026-09-07 pointer).** ADR-060 PR-1…6 all released to production (PR #124 then **PR #128** `staging`→`main`). Founder pointed a real custom affiliate domain **`fixfastapp.com`** at the storefront (as an `is_owned` brand) — it renders, but **live issues found, root-caused, fixes deferred** (full detail in the [[project_adr060_postdeploy_issues]] memory):

1. **ADR-060 custom-domain CORS gap — THE blocker, one bug with many symptoms.** `backend/config/cors.php` `allowed_origins` is static (`ADMIN_URL`/`STOREFRONT_URL`/`RESELLER_PORTAL_URL`) and doesn't cover custom affiliate domains. SSR reads render (no CORS), but **every `"use client"` call fails**: MLBB/player validation, checkout + preview-totals, `/membership` email-OTP ("takboleh send code"), the member dashboard/subscribe, `/track-order` ("Something went wrong — try again in a moment"), and the personalized packages fetch (so **neither standard nor member price shows**). Fix: a closure in `allowed_origins` checking the request `Origin` against `affiliate_domains WHERE status='active'` (+ configured URLs + `STOREFRONT_PRIMARY_HOSTS`) — safe, `supports_credentials:false`, guest API (ADR-011). **One fix clears all of the above.** Secondary (config, not a bug): a fresh `is_owned` affiliate has `membership_enabled = false` (default) — the founder must tick it in `/admin` for member pricing + the `/membership` link to appear ("own brand" ≠ auto membership parity, by ADR-061).
2. **Storefront-change propagation is 1-5s + needs multiple hard-refreshes** — the Vercel/Next layer, NOT our DB: `revalidateTag(CATALOG_TAG,"max")` is stale-while-revalidate (first hit stale, second fresh) + `PurgeNextCatalogCache` is queued + its `ShouldBeUnique(10s)` drops rapid successive purges. Fix candidates: `catalogCache.revalidate: 60` → `false` in `storefront/src/lib/cache.ts`; relax `ShouldBeUnique`.
3. **ADR-060 e2e still owed** — the custom-hostname/SSL flow is verified (`fixfastapp.com` live) but blocked from real end-to-end use by issue 1.
4. **`.env.example`** owes `STOREFRONT_PRIMARY_HOSTS=` + `VERCEL_*` + `GALLERY_DISK=` — founder `.env`/repo task (outside agent write scope).
5. **`PaymentMethodController::test()` probe returns a spurious HTTP 400 for `fpx`** — non-blocking, real checkout works. Send a fuller probe payload or drop the action.
6. **DuitNow QR + FPX B2B1** — separate later phases (`fpx_b2b1` / `duitnow_qr` rows seeded inactive; DuitNow QR still needs the hosted-page-vs-raw-QR render check, ADR-022 2026-09-01 decision 5).
7. **`MYSQL_ATTR_SSL_CA` hardening** — founder `.env` task only, skipped at launch; the link is already VPC-private + TLS.
8. **Supplier funding (Digiflazz + Gamevion, Rp 0)** — founder's deliberate hold, not a task to chase; revisit when the founder is ready to take real commercial orders.
9. **Real PekanGame logo asset + hero artwork** — the storefront placeholder mark is still in place; affiliate logos now upload via the portal but the primary's is still the SVG placeholder.
10. **Cloudflare R2 storage** — its own future ADR ([[project_cloudflare_r2_storage_backlog]]); PR-6's `ImageIngestService` + `GALLERY_DISK` config make it a config + one-off data migration. Fold in gallery→WebP + gallery-delete referential safety.

---

*This document serves as a blueprint for building the new system. v0.3 reflects a pre-build security/financial-integrity hardening pass — see `adr.md` for why each major decision was made, and `foundation-security.md` for the actionable checklist derived from those decisions. Technical details may evolve as architectural decisions are finalized, but any change to a recorded ADR should be deliberate and documented, not incidental.*
